Abstract: While social media platforms, such as Twitter, provide a medium for large-scale opinion sharing during news events, it is manually impossible for individuals or media agencies to process the vast volume of content to identify key viewpoints. In order to resolve this, several automatic summarization techniques have been proposed to condense large collections of tweets into concise and informative summaries. However, these algorithms do not explicitly consider demographic fairness. Several existing research works have developed automated summarization approaches that can provide a holistic overview of the key aspects and major opinions shared on social media platforms related to a news event. However, these approaches do not explicitly consider different forms of demographic representation, such as gender, which can lead to biased summary representation. In this paper, we propose EquiSumm, which considers the gender aspect of the shared opinion to generate a summary, and our experimental analysis on two major datasets indicates the performance effectiveness with respect to existing research works.

</details>


### [111] [Articulatory strategy as a source of variation in acoustic vowel dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23416)
*Patrycja Strycharczuk,Justin J. H. Lo,Sam Kirkham*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过超声舌位成像数据证明，发/i/音时不同的舌部构型策略会系统性地影响双元音中腭滑音的共振峰动态，揭示了发音补偿中的规律性与个体性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽有理论认为共振峰动态反映个体发音策略，但缺乏直接证据表明不同发音策略会系统性地影响共振峰动态。

Method: 采集36位北盎格鲁英语发音人的超声舌位成像数据，识别发/i/音时不同的舌部构型策略，并分析其与双元音中腭滑音共振峰动态的关系。

Result: 发现/i/音的舌形是双元音腭滑音共振峰动态的显著预测因子；舌根/舌背位移越大，腭元音舌形畸变越大，发音速度越高，导致共振峰过渡更早、更陡。

Conclusion: 研究结果阐明了发音补偿中规律性与个体性的交互作用，增进了对语音个体性的理解。

Abstract: Acoustic vowel dynamics have some speaker-identifying characteristics, which have been ascribed to individual properties of articulatory strategies: formant transitions have a particular shape because speakers move their articulators, using specific and practised movements. However, there is little existing evidence that different articulatory strategies systematically affect formant dynamics. The present study corroborates the link between the two. Ultrasound tongue imaging data from 36 speakers of Northern-Anglo English are used to identify distinct articulatory strategies for the production of palatal vowel /i/. Tongue shape in /i/ is found to be a significant predictor of formant dynamics in diphthongs with a palatal offglide. The observed relationships can be explained by the characteristics of articulatory movement conditioned by vocal tract shape. Greater articulatory displacement of tongue root and/or dorsum produces greater distortion from the mean tongue shape in palatal vowels, and it also requires higher articulatory velocities, resulting in relatively earlier and steeper formant transitions. The results contribute to the conceptual understanding of individuality in speech, by illuminating the regularising and individual aspects of articulatory compensation.

</details>


### [112] [Naturalistic measure of social norms alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23420)
*Yevhen Kostiuk,Kenneth Enevoldsen,Peter Bjerregaard Vahlstrup,Márton Kardos,Kristoffer Nielbo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在自然、自由形式的对话环境中衡量社会规范一致性的框架，通过解决方案匹配方法，并引入两个度量标准（陈述一致性和明确一致性），基于3k丹麦语社会困境数据集，评估了多种LLM与人类回答的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社会规范一致性测量依赖封闭式问卷或预定义陈述，无法捕捉真实自然场景中的社会规范。作者旨在解决开放、自然对话中社会规范对齐的测量难题。

Method: 提出基于解决方案匹配（solution matching）的框架，支持任意两个困境回应间的对齐测量（如LLM-人类、LLM-LLM、人类-人类）。引入两个指标：陈述一致性准确率（stated agreement accuracy）和明确一致性准确率（explicit agreement accuracy）。构建包含3k个非平凡丹麦语社会困境的数据集，每个困境由三名评审员（文化裁判）分配参考解决方案。

Result: 在类似自然用户-模型对话的交互设置中评估了多个LLM和人类回应。两个指标产生一致的模型排名，并揭示了不同困境类型间的一致性差异：在邻里冲突和共享居住等话题上一致性更高。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了一个用于研究自然开放对话中文化嵌入的社会推理的数据集和评估框架。

Abstract: Social norms reflect shared expectations on acceptable behavior. Measuring social norms alignment remains challenging, with existing approaches typically relying on artificial closed-form evaluations such as multiple-choice questionnaires or measuring agreement with predefined statements. In the context of this work, social norms alignment refers to measuring an agreement between solutions with respect to the social problem or dilemma. We propose a framework for measuring social norm alignment in naturalistic, free-form settings through solution matching. The framework enables us to measure alignment between any two dilemma responses e.g., LLMs to a human, LLMs to LLMs, or human to human. We introduce two metrics: stated and explicit agreement accuracy, and construct a dataset of 3k non-trivial social dilemmas in Danish. All dilemmas are assigned reference solutions derived from three panelists, who serve as culturally grounded judges. We evaluate the agreement of several LLMs and human responses in an interaction setup that resembles natural user-model conversations. Our results show that the proposed metrics produce consistent model rankings and reveal variation in agreement across different types of dilemmas, with higher agreement observed for topics such as neighbor conflicts and shared living situations. Overall, our work introduces a dataset and evaluation framework for studying culturally grounded social reasoning in naturalistic open-ended conversations.

</details>


### [113] [SSDAU: Structured Semantic Data Augmentation for Joint Entity and Relation Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23440)
*Jiawei He,Mengyu Shi,Chunrong Fang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出结构化语义数据增强(SSDAU)，通过保留文本语义结构生成增强数据，在实体关系联合抽取任务中显著优于现有方法，鲁棒性提升(模糊性下F1仅降8.26% vs 基线31.91%)


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据增强方法忽视文本相关性，可能破坏语义结构和依赖关系，导致生成的增强数据难以有效提升模型泛化能力

Method: 1) 基于实体标签分割文本 2) 编码器通过上下文感知捕获实体语义特征 3) 实体语义重组生成增强数据 4) 融合上下文嵌入与相似度分数区分语义相似实体 5) BERTTopic模型过滤无关主题保证一致性

Result: 在5个JERE模型和7个数据增强基线对比中，SSDAU在所有指标上显著优于现有方法，模糊性场景下F1仅下降8.26%（基线平均下降31.91%）

Conclusion: SSDAU能生成语义一致的增强数据，有效提升联合实体关系抽取模型的泛化能力和鲁棒性

Abstract: Joint Entity and Relation Extraction (JERE) is highly susceptible to weak generalization due to low-quality training data.
  Data augmentation is a common strategy to enhance model generalization across different domains.
  However, existing data augmentation methods often overlook text relevance and may disrupt semantic structures and dependencies, making it difficult to generate effective augmented data for improving model generalization.
  In this paper, we propose Structured Semantic Data Augmentation (SSDAU), a novel method designed to preserve the semantic structure of text during augmentation.
  SSDAU segments text based on entity labels and employs an encoder to capture semantic features of entities through context awareness.
  It then performs entity semantic restructuring to generate augmented data.
  To distinguish semantically similar entities, SSDAU fuses contextualized embeddings with traditional similarity scores.
  To mitigate potential topic ambiguity and information loss, we apply the BERTTopic model to filter out irrelevant topics, ensuring topic consistency.
  We evaluate SSDAU on datasets with different annotation types and compare its performance on five representative JERE models against seven popular data augmentation baselines.
  Experiments demonstrate that SSDAU generates semantically consistent data with superior robustness against ambiguity (8.26\% F1 decrease vs.\ 31.91\% for baselines), significantly outperforming all existing methods across all metrics.

</details>


### [114] [ARES: Automated Rubric Synthesis for Scalable LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23454)
*Xiaoyuan Li,Keqin Bao,Moxin Li,Yubo Ma,Yichang Zhang,Wenjie Wang,Fuli Feng,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ARES是一个自动化构建基于rubric的强化学习数据的框架，能从原始预训练文档中生成自包含的问答对及问题特定的加权rubric，实现实例级别的奖励监督，实验表明其在多维度开放任务上优于持续预训练、监督微调和二元奖励强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于rubric的强化学习在扩展时面临挑战：通常依赖专家编写的rubric和人工构建的问题集，且固定的任务级rubric无法捕捉单个问题的评估需求，需要一种自动化、可扩展的rubric构建方法。

Method: 提出ARES框架，从原始预训练文档出发，将源知识转换为自包含的问答对，并共同生成问题特定的加权rubric；通过领域标签和人物信息条件化生成来提升多样性和质量，并应用验证过滤器确保问题自包含性、答案忠实性和rubric有效性。

Result: 跨十个领域构建了10万个rubric标注实例，在七个基准测试上，基于ARES的rubric奖励训练在持续预训练、监督微调和二元奖励强化学习上均表现更优，尤其在医疗和指令遵循等多维开放任务上提升最大。

Conclusion: ARES为大规模自动构建rubric奖励数据提供了一种有效框架，能够实例级别地为开放答案提供奖励监督，且实验验证了其相对于多种基线方法的优势。

Abstract: Rubric-based rewards offer a promising way to extend reinforcement learning (RL) for large language models beyond tasks with automatically verifiable answers. However, scaling rubric-based RL remains challenging: existing approaches often rely on expert-written rubrics and manually constructed question sets, while fixed task-level rubrics may fail to capture the evaluation requirements of individual questions. We propose ARES (Automated Rubric synthEsis for Scalable RL), a framework for automatically constructing rubric-based RL data at scale. Starting from raw pretraining documents, ARES converts source knowledge into self-contained question-answer pairs and co-generates question-specific weighted rubrics, enabling instance-level reward supervision for open-ended responses. To improve diversity and quality, ARES conditions generation on domain labels and persona information, and applies validation filters for question self-containment, answer faithfulness, and rubric validity. Using ARES, we construct 100K rubric-annotated instances across ten domains. Experiments on seven benchmarks show that rubric-based RL trained with ARES, outperforms continual pretraining, supervised fine-tuning, and binary-reward RL, with the largest gains on multi-dimensional open-ended tasks such as healthcare and instruction following.

</details>


### [115] [Structure-Guided Entity Resolution: Fine-Tuning LLMs for Robust Name Matching in Complex Linguistic Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23597)
*Shivam Chourasia,Hitesh Kapoor,Nilesh Patil*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出结构引导的实体解析（SGER）框架，通过两阶段课程学习微调大型语言模型，先在姓名语法语义结构上训练，再进行二元实体匹配优化。在印度身份数据上达到99.02%准确率和0.994 F1分数，优于GPT-4o少量提示和单阶段微调基线，已在Dream11平台部署服务2.5亿+用户。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨异构记录匹配人名是实体解析的核心挑战，特别是在语言和文化复杂的环境中。命名惯例差异、跨文字音译不一致以及频繁的数据录入错误使得统一用户身份变得困难，而这又是了解你的客户（KYC）合规的基本要求。虽然大语言模型在理解自然语言方面表现出色，但它们往往难以处理此类特定领域中的结构化歧义。

Method: 提出结构引导的实体解析（SGER）框架，通过两阶段课程学习微调大语言模型：第一阶段训练模型解析个人姓名的语法和语义结构，第二阶段优化下游的二元实体匹配任务。

Result: 在包含50,000个真实世界配对的数据集上，SGER达到99.02%的准确率和0.994的F1分数，超越了GPT-4o少量提示和单阶段微调基线。该系统已在全球最大幻想体育平台Dream11投入生产，服务超过2.5亿用户。

Conclusion: 课程引导的训练能够在大规模真实多语言系统中实现稳健、高精度的实体解析。

Abstract: Matching person names across heterogeneous records is a core challenge in entity resolution, especially within linguistically and culturally complex environments. Variations in naming conventions, inconsistent transliteration across scripts, and frequent data entry errors make it difficult to unify user identities, an essential requirement for Know Your Customer (KYC) compliance. While Large Language Models have shown promise in understanding natural language, they often struggle with the structured ambiguity present in such domain-specific settings. This paper introduces Structure-Guided Entity Resolution (SGER), a novel framework that fine-tunes an LLM through a two-phase curriculum. The model is first trained to parse the grammatical and semantic structure of personal names, then optimized for the downstream task of binary entity matching. We evaluate SGER in the challenging context of Indian identity data, one of the most linguistically diverse and noisy environments globally. SGER achieves 99.02% accuracy and an F1 of 0.994 on a held-out set of 50,000 real-world pairs, outperforming GPT-4o few-shot prompting and single-stage fine-tuning baselines. The system is fully deployed in production at Dream11, the world's largest fantasy sports platform, serving 250M+ users. Our results demonstrate that curriculum-guided training enables robust, high-precision entity resolution in real-world multilingual systems at scale.

</details>


### [116] [Benchmarking Google Embeddings 2 against Open-Source Models for Multilingual Dense Retrieval and RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23618)
*Stefano Cirillo,Domenico Desiato,Giuseppe Polese,Giandomenico Solimando*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对Google Embeddings (GE2)与五种开源嵌入模型在BEIR子集、意大利语RAG语料库、分块消融实验和延迟基准上的全面评测，GE2在所有任务上排名第一，但延迟比最快本地模型高约14倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估新兴的闭源Google Embeddings (GE2)与主流开源替代方案在检索质量、多语言能力、分块策略和延迟上的实际性能差距，为实际部署提供选型参考。

Method: 在4个BEIR子集、合成意大利语RAG语料库上进行nDCG@10评测；开展5种分块大小×3种分块策略的消融实验；测量每查询的中位数延迟（CPU硬件）。

Result: GE2在BEIR上avg nDCG@10=0.638，IT-RAG-Bench上nDCG@10=0.282，但中位数延迟231.6ms，是最快本地模型的约14倍。mE5-L在意大利语任务上仅差0.003 nDCG且延迟31ms。LaBSE在BEIR上仅0.188 nDCG，低于所有专用检索模型。所有模型在32 token分块时饱和，语义分块仅在16 token时有可测量收益。

Conclusion: GE2质量最优但延迟代价高；mE5-L在延迟敏感场景下是性价比最高的替代方案；LaBSE在多语言检索任务中表现不佳，不推荐作为检索专用模型；32 token是通用分块大小阈值。该工作给出了清晰的模型选型与分块策略指南。

Abstract: We benchmark Google Embeddings (GE2), a Vertex-AI-hosted bi-encoder with 2,048-token context and explicit task-type conditioning, against five open-source alternatives: BGE-M3, E5-large, Multilingual-E5-large (mE5-L), LaBSE, and Paraphrase-Multilingual-MPNet (mMPNet). Evaluation covers four BEIR subsets, a synthetic Italian RAG corpus, a chunking ablation considering 5 sizes of tokens with three strategies, and per-query latency on commodity CPU hardware. GE2 ranks first on every task, achieving BEIR avg.nDCG@10 = 0.638 and IT-RAG-Bench nDCG@10 = 0.282, but at 231.6 ms median latency, it is roughly 14x slower than the fastest local models. mE5-L reaches within 0.003 nDCG of GE2 on Italian at 31 ms, making it the preferred option when sub-100 ms SLAs matter. A more striking finding concerns LaBSE, which, despite widespread multilingual deployment scores 0.188 average nDCG@10 on BEIR, below every dedicated retrieval model including mMPNet. Chunking experiments show that all six models saturate at 32-token chunks on our corpus, with semantic chunking providing measurable gains only at 16 tokens.

</details>


### [117] [How Human-Like Are Large Language Models? A Register-Aware Linguistic Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23651)
*Björn Nieth,Marianna Gracheva,Michaela Mahlberg,Bjoern Eskofier,Emmanuelle Salin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种上下文感知的评估框架，通过对比人类参考语料库和LLM生成语料库的词汇-语法特征分布，使用最大均值差异（MMD）和Biber的67个特征来评估文本的人类相似度。实验发现LLM生成的文本在语言学层面偏离人类基线，且与人类语言最接近的模型取决于语域而非模型大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，LLM研究主要关注事实正确性和任务表现，但忽略了生成文本在语言学层面上是否具有人类相似的特性。从语料库语言学角度看，语言生产具有上下文依赖性，不同交际语境会导致语言特征的频率和共现模式差异。如果文本不符合这些模式，即使内容正确，也可能让人类读者感到不自然。因此需要一种评估框架来量化LLM文本在语言学层面上的'人类相似度'。

Method: 提出上下文感知的评估框架，采用两样本问题方法，比较人类参考语料库（按语域划分）与LLM生成语料库之间的语言特征分布差异。实现上使用最大均值差异（MMD）作为统计度量，并采用Biber提出的67个词汇-语法特征（常用于语料库语言学）。实验比较了7个经过指令微调的开源模型，跨越5个不同语域的英文数据集，与人类基线进行对比。

Result: 在所有测试设置中，LLM生成的文本都偏离了人类基线。但哪个模型最接近人类语言取决于具体的语域（register），而不是由模型大小决定的。

Conclusion: LLM在语言学层面尚未完全达到人类水平，且不同语域下表现最佳的模型不同，说明需要针对特定语域进行优化，而非单纯扩大模型规模。

Abstract: While factual correctness and task-performance have been in focus of Large Language Model (LLM) research for a long time, the fundamental question of how human-like generated texts are on a linguistic level has been underexplored. From a corpus-linguistic perspective, language production is inherently context-dependent, with distinct communicative contexts giving rise to differences in frequencies and co-occurrence patterns of linguistic features. A text failing to adhere to these patterns can be content-wise correct, but still be unfavorable to human readers. In this work, we propose a context-aware evaluation framework in which human-likeness is assessed using a two-sample problem between the linguistic feature distribution of a human reference corpus for a given register and a corresponding LLM-generated corpus. We implement this framework using the Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) and the 67 lexico-grammatical features introduced by Biber, which are commonly applied in corpus linguistics. In our experiments, we compare seven instruction-tuned, open-source models across five English-language datasets spanning distinct registers against a human baseline. While across all tested setups, LLMs deviate from the human baseline, which models are closest to human language depends on the register and is not dictated by model size.

</details>


### [118] [OpenSkillEval: Automatically Auditing the Open Skill Ecosystem for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23657)
*Jiahao Ying,Boxian Ai,Wei Tang,Siyuan Liu,Yixin Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了OpenSkillEval，一个自动化评估框架，用于评估技能增强的LLM智能体系统及技能本身。通过600+动态任务实例和30个开源技能，发现技能可用性不保证有效使用，技能提升效果依赖模型和框架，很多热门技能并不比无技能的基座智能体表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着开源技能生态系统快速扩张，不同模型和智能体框架如何与技能交互、如何评估技能质量、用户如何在成本-性能权衡下选择技能等问题尚未清晰。现有方法依赖静态基准，无法反映实际应用中的动态性和复杂性。

Method: 提出自动评估框架OpenSkillEval，利用不断演化的真实世界工件自动构建五个下游应用类别（演示文稿生成、前端网页设计、海报生成、数据可视化和报告生成）的逼真任务实例，并收集整理社区贡献的技能，在统一任务设置下进行受控比较。

Result: 基于600+动态任务实例和30个开源技能的系统评估表明：技能可用性并不保证有效使用；技能增强的收益强烈依赖于底层模型和智能体框架；许多公开流行的技能并不一致地优于没有技能的基座智能体。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了动态、任务驱动的评估的必要性，并为LLM智能体中技能的设计、选择与部署提供了实用洞见。

Abstract: Skills, i.e., structured workflow instructions distilled for large language models (LLMs), are becoming an increasingly important mechanism for improving agent performance on real-world downstream tasks. However, as the open-source skill ecosystem rapidly expands, it remains unclear how different models and agent frameworks interact with skills, how to evaluate skill quality, and how users should select skills under practical cost-performance trade-offs. In this paper, we present \textsc{OpenSkillEval}, an automatic evaluation framework for both skill-augmented agent systems and the skills themselves. Instead of relying on static benchmarks, \textsc{OpenSkillEval} automatically constructs realistic task instances from evolving real-world artifacts across five categories of downstream applications: presentation generation, front-end web design, poster generation, data visualization, and report generation. It further collects and organizes community-contributed skills for controlled comparison under unified task settings. Using more than 600 dynamically generated task instances and 30 open-source skills, we conduct a systematic evaluation of state-of-the-art models and agent frameworks. Our results show that skill availability does not guarantee effective skill usage, that the benefit of skill augmentation depends strongly on both the underlying model and the agent framework, and that many publicly popular skills do not consistently outperform base agents without skills. These findings highlight the need for dynamic, task-grounded evaluation and provide practical insights into the design, selection, and deployment of skills for LLM agents. Additional cases and benchmark resources are available on the project website: https://yingjiahao14.github.io/OpenSkillEval-Web/.

</details>


### [119] [OnePred: Next-Query Prediction via Recursive Intent Memory in Multi-Turn Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23668)
*Jiangwang Chen,Bowen Zhang,Zixin Song,Jiazheng Kang,Xiao Yang,Da Zhu,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出OnePred方法，通过递归更新记忆作为唯一跨轮次上下文，实现高效准确的下一轮查询预测，将每轮token消耗减少最多22倍，并在预测质量上超越所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM对话系统本质上是被动的，需要用户输入后才响应。实现主动交互的关键是下一轮查询预测，但缺乏专用基准且存在效率-质量权衡：全对话历史拼接导致token消耗线性增长，而截断会丢失跨轮次上下文。

Method: 提出OnePred，维护递归更新的记忆作为唯一跨轮次上下文，使每轮成本与对话长度无关；通过两阶段强化学习流水线训练模型：先学习预测什么，再学习压缩什么，将记忆塑造成面向预测的意图链。

Result: 在自建基准NQP-Bench的三个子集上，OnePred相比全历史输入将每轮token消耗减少最多22倍，同时预测质量持续超越所有基线，在长对话上增益更大。

Conclusion: OnePred通过维护压缩的递归记忆实现了高效的下一轮查询预测，解决了效率与质量的根本权衡，代码已开源。

Abstract: Although large language model (LLM) conversational systems process millions of multi-turn dialogues daily, they remain fundamentally reactive: they respond only after the user types a query. A key step toward proactive interaction is next-query prediction, which anticipates the user's subsequent query based solely on the preceding dialogue. Progress on this task is hindered by the lack of dedicated benchmarks and a fundamental efficiency--quality trade-off: naively concatenating full dialogue history incurs linearly growing token consumption, while truncating to the latest turn discards crucial cross-turn context. Our key insight is that accurate prediction does not require re-reading raw history; it suffices to track the user's evolving intent trajectory across topics, unresolved needs, and interest shifts. We propose OnePred, which maintains a recursively updated memory as its sole cross-turn context, bounding the per-turn cost independently of conversation length. We train the model via a two-stage reinforcement learning pipeline that first teaches what to predict, then what to compress, shaping the memory into a prediction-oriented intent chain. To establish a rigorous testbed, we introduce NQP-Bench, spanning three diverse subsets. Experiments demonstrate that OnePred reduces per-turn token consumption by up to 22$\times$ compared to full-history inputs while consistently exceeding all baselines in prediction quality, with larger gains on longer conversations. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/ZBWpro/OnePred.

</details>


### [120] [ChartFI: Benchmarking Faithfulness and Insightfulness of Chart Descriptions from Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23694)
*Fen Wang,Zekai Shao,Qiman Kang,Chunran Hu,Zhixuan Zhang,Lexu Xie,Chao Liu,Siming Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了ChartFI-Bench基准，从忠实度和洞察力四个维度评估多模态大模型生成图表描述的质量，并设计了对应的四个评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表描述基准存在两个缺陷：数据集包含简单同质化的图表配以浅显的事实列举式描述；现有指标无法捕捉描述质量的多面性。

Method: 首先总结高质量图表描述的四个维度（事实准确性、显著特征强调、领域指导、图表-文本互补），构建包含896个图表-描述对的基准（复杂图表+语义丰富描述），并设计四个对齐的评估指标（忠实度、覆盖率、信息量、敏锐度）。

Result: 在主流多模态大模型上的实验证明了框架的有效性，揭示了现有模型的常见弱点。

Conclusion: ChartFI-Bench能够系统评估图表描述质量，识别MLLMs在生成忠实且富有洞察力的图表描述方面的不足。

Abstract: Chart descriptions are essential for accessibility, cross-modal retrieval, and assisting readers in extracting insights from complex visualizations. As multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly adopted for automated chart description generation, a critical question arises: how faithfully and insightfully do these models actually describe charts? Current benchmarks fall short on two fronts: existing datasets consist of simple, homogeneous charts paired with shallow, fact-enumerating descriptions; and prevailing metrics fail to capture the multi-faceted nature of description quality. To address these gaps, we present the Chart Faithfulness and Insightfulness Benchmark (ChartFI-Bench). We first summarize four dimensions that characterize high-quality chart descriptions: factual accuracy, salient feature emphasis, domain-informed guidance, and chart-text complementarity. Guided by these dimensions, we construct a high-quality benchmark comprising 896 chart-description pairs, which feature visually complex charts and semantically rich descriptions. Furthermore, we design four aligned evaluation metrics -- Faithfulness, Coverage, Informativeness, and Acuity -- to systematically assess the quality of descriptions across these dimensions. Experiments conducted on mainstream MLLMs demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework and reveal common weaknesses among existing models.

</details>


### [121] [Metadata Predictability Is Not Evidence Dependence: An Intervention-Based Audit for Weak-Label Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23701)
*Kan Shao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套三重审计协议（元数据统计+证据干预+阅读器校准），用于检测弱标签基准测试中模型是否真正依赖证据而非元数据捷径，并通过合成HotpotQA、SNLI、FEVER等案例验证了单独使用元数据筛查的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弱标签基准测试的评估方法存在缺陷：仅通过元数据统计（如MPDS）判断模型是否依赖捷径，但这种方法无法区分模型是否真的使用了证据。作者希望建立一个更全面的测试框架，同时检测元数据主导性、证据敏感性和阅读器强度校准效应。

Method: 组合三个统计量：1) 元数据先验主导分数MPDS（衡量输出是否可从元数据预测）；2) 证据干预统计量ΔEvi（通过跨样本打乱证据衡量对证据身份的真实敏感性）；3) 阅读器强度校准（在不同能力水平的模型上复现以发现反转效应）。在合成HotpotQA、SNLI、重构HotpotQA和FEVER四个基准上验证。

Result: 合成HotpotQA展示了MPDS中等（0.643）但ΔEvi为零的反例；SNLI出现校准反转效应；重构HotpotQA呈现问题主导的警告区域；FEVER在所有四种Transformer上均表现为强证据敏感的正对照。

Conclusion: 基准审计应同时报告三个维度：元数据筛查、证据干预和阅读器强度校准，单独依赖元数据统计可能掩盖模型实际不依赖证据的问题。

Abstract: We study a protocol-level test for weak-label benchmarks: whether benchmark outputs change when the provided evidence is intervened on. Metadata-only shortcut checks answer a different question, namely whether outputs are predictable from metadata priors. We therefore combine a metadata statistic, the Metadata Prior Dominance Score (MPDS), with an evidence-intervention statistic, ΔEvi, measuring sensitivity to evidence identity under cross-item shuffling. Synthetic HotpotQA gives a constructed counterexample to metadata-only screening: MPDS is only moderate (0.643), yet ΔEvi is zero. Stronger-reader reruns show why calibration belongs in the test procedure: SNLI shows a calibration reversal, reconstructed HotpotQA occupies a question-dominant warning region, and FEVER is a strongly evidence-sensitive positive control across four transformers. The practical lesson is simple: benchmark audits should report metadata-only screening, evidence intervention, and reader-strength calibration together.

</details>


### [122] [A graph-based analysis of semantic types and coercion in contextualized word embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23710)
*Long Chen,Deniz Ekin Yavas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图的方法，利用BERT和感知增强的嵌入来研究名词与其上下文之间的语义类型匹配/不匹配（包括强制现象），并提出了两个指标（邻域类型概率NTP和邻域类型熵NTE）来区分匹配与不匹配的句子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决语义类型强制（coercion）现象中名词与其上下文之间的语义类型不匹配问题，探究词汇和上下文类型信息在词嵌入中的反映方式。

Method: 选取10个语义类型的名词，对语料实例进行类型匹配标注（匹配/强制/其他不匹配/无限制）；使用BERT和感知增强的嵌入构建图；提出两个度量指标——邻域类型概率（NTP）和邻域类型熵（NTE）——来分析邻域类型分布。

Result: 使用感知增强嵌入构建的图能更好地反映语义类型信息；通过提出的NTP和NTE指标可以有效区分匹配和不匹配的句子。

Conclusion: 图方法和新提出的度量指标能够有效捕捉和区分语义类型匹配与不匹配（包括强制）现象，感知增强嵌入在反映语义类型信息方面优于标准BERT嵌入。

Abstract: Semantic type mismatch between a noun and its context is central to coercion phenomena. This paper introduces a graph-based method to examine how lexical and contextual type information is reflected in word embeddings. We select nouns from ten semantic types, annotate corpus instances for type matching (matching vs. coercion vs. other mismatch vs. unrestricted), and construct graphs using BERT and sense-enhanced embeddings. Two metrics -- Neighbor Type Probability (NTP) and Neighbor Type Entropy (NTE) -- are proposed to analyze neighborhood type distributions. Results show that graphs constructed with sense-enhanced embeddings reflect semantic type information better, and matching and mismatch sentences can be distinguished through the proposed metrics.

</details>


### [123] [NLG Evaluation: Past, Present, Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23715)
*Ehud Reiter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文回顾了NLG评估从1990年到2026年的演变历程，指出评估方式从早期与语言学紧密相关时的非正式评价，发展到如今与机器学习紧密结合的实验性评估（如LLM-as-Judge），并预测未来影响评估、定性评估和安全评估将变得越发重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统梳理NLG评估36年来的发展脉络，厘清评估范式的转变趋势，并为未来评估方向提供前瞻性指导。

Method: 历史回顾与趋势分析，通过对比1990年和2026年两个时间节点的NLG评估特征，结合技术发展（机器学习、LLM-as-Judge）进行推演。

Result: 识别出NLG评估从语言学导向的非实验性评价转向机器学习驱动的实验性评价，并发现了LLM-as-Judge等新兴技术。

Conclusion: 未来NLG评估将更加重视影响评估、定性评估和安全评估，因为这些方面对大规模日常使用的NLG技术至关重要。

Abstract: Natural Language Generation (NLG) evaluation has changed dramatically since 1990, and will continue to evolve in the future. In 1990, when NLG had close ties to linguistics, there was very little formal experimental evaluation in the modern sense. In 2026, when NLG is closely linked to machine learning, experimental evaluation is expected and indeed fundamental to research. Many evaluation techniques were developed over this period, including most recently LLM-as-Judge. I expect NLG evaluation will continue to evolve in the future. In particular, impact, qualitative, and safety evaluation will become more important as large numbers of people routinely use NLG technology.

</details>


### [124] [Is a Document Educational or Just Wikipedia-Style? -- Pitfalls of Classifier-Based Quality Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23721)
*Mateusz Klimaszewski,Piotr Andruszkiewicz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了基于分类器的质量过滤（CQF）方法存在关键漏洞：对文档进行简单的Wikipedia式格式重排就能显著改变模型的质量评估结果，导致低质量内容绕过过滤阈值。在FineWeb-Edu CQF模型上，约7%的文档会被反转过滤决策，从而让原本被排除的低质量内容混入预训练语料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于分类器的质量过滤在构建预训练语料中已成为基础技术，但研究者怀疑其存在系统性漏洞，即过度依赖表面格式特征而非真正的语义质量，缺乏对格式变换鲁棒性的验证。

Method: 设计并实施Wikipedia式格式重排操作（如重构标题结构、调整段落顺序、规范化引用格式等），在FineWeb-Edu CQF模型上系统性地评估格式变换前后对质量评分和过滤决策的影响。

Result: 格式重排导致约7%的文档质量评分出现跨越阈值的反转（从'低质量'变为'高质量'），使得这些本应被过滤的低质量内容得以混入预训练语料。

Conclusion: 当前主流的分类器质量过滤方法存在严重脆弱性，对文档的表面格式高度敏感。建议在构建预训练语料时，对质量过滤模型进行格式鲁棒性测试，或结合多维度特征（如来源可信度、内容多样性）共同决策以缓解此类漏洞。

Abstract: Classifier-based Quality Filtering has recently emerged as a fundamental technique in constructing pre-training corpora. The ability to deploy a single model that can replace or supplement a set of heuristics has proven effective across numerous Large Language Models. In this work, we expose a critical vulnerability in this approach by demonstrating how a straightforward Wikipedia-style reformatting operation can substantially alter a model's quality assessment and enable low-quality content to surpass filtering thresholds. Our analysis reveals that the FineWeb-Edu CQF model would reverse its filtering decision for approximately 7% of evaluated documents, thereby admitting content into the pre-training corpus that would otherwise have been excluded.

</details>


### [125] [Hierarchical Concept Geometry in Language Models Emerges from Word Co-occurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23821)
*Andres Nava,Matthieu Wyart*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分布性理论，证明词嵌入中上下位关系（"is-a"）的层级几何结构源于共现统计的谱分解，而非层级专用机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言模型中层级概念几何（如超名词关系）的编码机制，探究其是否源于层级特定功能或统计分布的自然涌现。

Method: 基于WordNet超名词图假设（邻近词共现更频繁），理论刻画word2vec嵌入Gram矩阵的谱特征，在共现核的弱正性及衰减条件下，证明主导特征向量先分离粗粒度分类支系，再逐步细化子支系。

Result: 在多个WordNet子树的word2vec嵌入中验证理论预测，且该谱签名在Gemma 2B模型的去嵌入层中同样显著存在。

Conclusion: LLM中层级概念几何无需依赖层级特定功能机制，而是从成对词统计的谱结构中涌现而出。

Abstract: We propose a distributional theory of how hypernymy -- the ``is-a'' relation between general and specific concepts -- is encoded geometrically in language representations. Starting from the empirically verified assumption that words closer on the WordNet hypernym graph co-occur more often, we characterize theoretically the spectrum of the resulting embedding Gram matrix of word2vec embeddings. Under mild positivity and decay conditions on the co-occurrence kernel, we prove that the leading eigenvectors first separate broad taxonomic branches and then progressively finer sub-branches, producing a \emph{hierarchical splitting geometry} with a coarse-to-fine spectral organization that mirrors the tree. We confirm these predictions in word2vec embeddings across many sampled WordNet subtrees, and show that the same signature extends strikingly well to Gemma 2B unembeddings. Our results indicate that hierarchical concept geometry in LLMs need not reflect a hierarchy-specific functional mechanism, but emerges from the spectral structure of pairwise word statistics.

</details>


### [126] [Multilingual Knowledge Transfer under Data Constraints via Lexical Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23885)
*Anastasiia Sedova,Natalie Schluter,Skyler Seto,Maartje ter Hoeve*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LINK是一种数据级干预方法，通过在预训练阶段对高资源语言（英语）数据进行词汇替换（使用双语词汇表），实现向低资源语言的跨语言知识迁移，无需平行语料或额外训练，在8种语言上取得显著效果，最高可达2倍训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低资源语言训练数据不足时，许多下游任务（科学推理、常识推理、世界知识）所需的知识主要来自高资源语言，现有跨语言知识迁移方法依赖大量平行数据、翻译系统、辅助模型或额外训练阶段，这些资源对许多语言不可用。

Method: 提出LINK方法，在模型预训练阶段对高资源语言（英语）训练数据中的部分单词，按给定替换比例随机用双语词汇表中的词级翻译进行替换，仅需双语词汇表（几乎零成本获取），无需额外模型训练。

Result: 在8种语言、5种模型规模上的评估显示，目标语言下游任务性能显著提升，训练速度最高可提升2倍达到同等性能水平。

Conclusion: LINK通过简单的数据级词汇替换干预，有效实现了跨语言知识迁移，方法轻量、资源要求低，适用于几乎任何语言，显著提升了低资源语言模型的训练效率与性能。

Abstract: Cross-lingual knowledge transfer is critical for building high-performing multilingual language models for languages with insufficient training data. When target language data is scarce, the knowledge required for many downstream tasks involving scientific reasoning, commonsense inference, and world knowledge must be acquired primarily from the high-resource language, making effective knowledge transfer essential. Existing methods for improving such cross-lingual knowledge transfer require large amounts of parallel data, translation systems, auxiliary models, or additional training stages that are largely unavailable for many languages. We propose LINK - a data-level intervention method that improves knowledge transfer during model pretraining through lexical substitutions in high-resource part of pretraining data using bilingual vocabularies. For a given replacement ratio, randomly selected words in a portion of the high-resource (English) training corpus are swapped with their word-level translations, requiring no additional model training and only a bilingual vocabulary, which can be obtained at near-zero cost for virtually any language. Evaluation on eight languages across five model sizes shows notable improvements on downstream tasks in the target language, with up to a 2x speedup in training to reach equivalent performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Swarical: An Integrated Hierarchical Approach to Localizing Flying Light Specks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23774)
*Hamed Alimohammadzadeh,Shahram Ghandeharizadeh*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: Swarical是一种基于蜂群的层次化定位技术，使微型无人机（FLS）能够利用异构传感器混搭和硬件规范转换的网格文件点云，以最高精度和超过2倍于现有技术的速度定位并照亮复杂2D/3D形状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决微型无人机在复杂形状照明任务中，如何利用有限硬件（传感器）实现高精度、高效率的分布式定位问题，并确保异构传感器间保持视距。

Method: 采用层次化蜂群定位：通过硬件规范将网格文件转换为点云；利用异构FLS（不同传感器朝向）保证视距；以Raspberry相机和ArUco标记实现。

Result: 与最先进的去中心化定位技术相比，精度相当但速度提升超过2倍。

Conclusion: Swarical在保持精度的前提下显著提升了定位速度，为微型无人机群体在复杂形状照明中提供了高效的定位方案。

Abstract: Swarical, a Swarm-based hierarchical localization technique, enables miniature drones, known as Flying Light Specks (FLSs), to accurately and efficiently localize and illuminate complex 2D and 3D shapes. Its accuracy depends on the physical hardware (sensors) of FLSs, which are used to track neighboring FLSs in order to localize themselves. It uses the hardware specification to convert mesh files into point clouds that enable a swarm of FLSs to localize at the highest accuracy afforded by their hardware. Swarical considers a heterogeneous mix of FLSs with different orientations for their tracking sensors, ensuring a line of sight between a localizing FLS and its anchor FLS. We present an implementation using Raspberry cameras and ArUco markers. A comparison of Swarical with a state of the art decentralized localization technique shows that it is as accurate and more than 2x faster.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [Reading Calibrated Uncertainty from Language Model Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22864)
*Aliai Eusebi,Alexander Herzog,Xiaoyu Liang,Marie Vasek,Enrico Mariconti,Lorenzo Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 利用层间几何特征追踪MLP更新的累积路径，通过稀疏线性探针提升语言模型结构化输出时的不确定性校准，比MSP方法在选择性弃权上提升最高21 AURC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大softmax概率（MSP）虽简单但校准差，且现有内部激活探针将隐藏状态视为静态快照，忽略了表示形成的逐层轨迹，而相似终点可能源于不同路径，层间证据的累积、强化或反转可揭示最终概率掩盖的不确定性。

Method: 提取11个尺度不变的几何特征，追踪每层MLP更新的累积路径，将其输入稀疏线性探针进行不确定性估计。

Result: 在选择性弃权任务中，该方法优于MSP，增益随基线校准误差增大而提升，最高可达21 AURC点。

Conclusion: 每个特征具有封闭形式的几何意义，探针系数可追溯误差在深度中形成的位置和方式，揭示哪些层过早定论、哪些层与运行状态矛盾、轨迹何时偏离终点。

Abstract: The maximum softmax probability (MSP) represents a default approach when evaluating uncertainty quantification for language model generation with structured output. Although cheap, it is often miscalibrated. Methods that probe the model's internal activations feed raw hidden states into opaque classifiers, reading activations as static snapshots and leaving implicit the layer-wise trajectory by which a representation is formed. Yet, similar endpoints can arise from very different paths, and how evidence accumulates, reinforces, or reverses across depth might reveal uncertainty that final probabilities obscure. We extract eleven scale-invariant geometric features, tracing the cumulative path of per-layer MLP updates, and feed them to a sparse linear probe. The probe outperforms MSP under selective abstention, with gains scaling with baseline miscalibration up to 21 AURC points. Because every feature has a closed-form geometric meaning, the probe's coefficients trace how and where along depth errors take shape -- which layers commit prematurely, which contradict the running state, where trajectories drift away from their endpoint.

</details>


### [129] [FusionSense: Tri-Stage Near-Sensor Learning for Runtime-Adaptive Multimodal Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22868)
*Sanggeon Yun,Ryozo Masukawa,Minhyoung Na,Hyunwoo Oh,Yoshiki Yamaguchi,Wenjun Huang,SungHeon Jeong,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FusionSense提出一种融合感知的智能传感框架，通过三步训练（服务器端融合模型学习→生成过滤安全标签→压缩边缘融合模型），在能量受限的自主边缘系统中联合降低计算与通信开销，在RGB+Depth双模态无人机数据集上实现最高33倍能耗降低、92.3%质量损失削减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么在强大服务器上融合多模态数据，要么应用忽略跨模态依赖的单模态近传感器滤波器，导致冗余传输或事件遗漏。随着多模态传感器套件在边缘端激增，迫切需要一种能感知融合的轻量级框架来同时满足能量、延迟和可靠性约束。

Method: 三步训练流程：(i)在服务器端训练融合模型学习下游任务；(ii)生成'过滤安全'(FoS)标签量化各模态相对于融合决策的必要性；(iii)将近传感器预测作为辅助信号注入，压缩边缘端融合模型。运行时决策层联合降低计算与通信，开销随传感器数量线性扩展。

Result: 在双模态(RGB+Depth/LiDAR)SynDrone数据集上：1%感兴趣目标出现率下节能33倍，10%下节能11倍；固定30%数据缩减时质量损失降低92.3%；能耗节省比最佳基线高约1.5倍。

Conclusion: FusionSense通过融合感知的智能传感设计，在保持任务质量的同时实现了显著高于单模态滤波器的数据缩减率和端到端能效提升，验证了跨模态依赖建模在边缘智能中的关键价值。

Abstract: Autonomous systems and smart-industry deployments increasingly split computation across near-sensor, edge, and cloud resources, where tight energy, latency, and reliability budgets demand run-time adaptivity. In practice, deciding what to compute and transmit at each point is pivotal; yet as multimodal sensor suites (cameras, LiDAR/depth, etc.) proliferate at the edge, most prior approaches either (i) fuse modalities on powerful servers or (ii) apply uni-modal near-sensor filters that ignore cross-modal dependencies, leading to redundant transmissions or missed events. We present FusionSense, a fusion-aware intelligent sensing framework for energy-constrained autonomous edge systems. Lightweight near-sensor classifiers are trained via a three-step procedure: (i) a server-side fusion model learns the downstream task, (ii) filter-out-safe (FoS) labels quantify each modality's necessity relative to the fused decision, and (iii) an edge-side fusion model is compacted by injecting near-sensor predictions as auxiliary signals. The result is a run-time decision layer that jointly reduces compute and communication while scaling linearly with sensor count. On a dual-modality (RGB+Depth/LiDAR) setup with SynDrone, FusionSense sustains task quality at substantially higher data-reduction rates than uni-modal filters and delivers large end-to-end gains: up to 33x lower energy at 1% FoI prevalence, 11x at 10%, a 92.3% reduction in quality loss at a fixed 30% data reduction, and roughly 1.5x higher energy savings than the best prior filtering baseline.

</details>


### [130] [FuRA: Full-Rank Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning with Spectral Preconditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22869)
*Yequan Zhao,Ruijie Zhang,Liyan Tan,Niall Moran,Tong Qin,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FuRA（全秩适应）方法，通过基于SVD的谱预处理和块张量训练分解，在保持全秩更新能力的同时实现参数高效的微调，在多项任务中超越全微调和LoRA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微调方法（全微调、LoRA等）在更新权重时未考虑预训练建立的谱结构，导致有限微调数据的噪声梯度可能干扰鲁棒的预训练特征。因此需要一种能保持预训练谱结构特性的微调方法。

Method: 通过SVD分解将每个权重矩阵重新参数化，冻结一个奇异基以约束更新到预训练列空间；提出基于块张量训练分解W=LSR的FuRA框架，固定大核心L为预训练的块SVD基，仅优化紧凑核心R和块奇异值S，实现全秩谱预处理和全秩更新。

Result: 在LLM微调（LLaMA-3-8B常识推理+1.37）、LLM强化学习数学推理、VLM视觉指令调优等多场景中一致超越全微调；4位量化变体QFuRA也超越QLoRA。

Conclusion: FuRA通过谱预处理实现了全秩适应的参数效率、内存和训练速度接近LoRA，同时性能超越全微调，为参数高效微调提供了新范式。

Abstract: Both full fine-tuning (Full FT) and parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods such as LoRA introduce weight updates without accounting for the spectral structure established during pretraining. As a result, noisy gradients from limited fine-tuning data can perturb robust pretrained features. We identify spectral preconditioning as the missing ingredient: reparameterizing each weight matrix through its full-rank singular value decomposition (SVD) and freezing one singular basis constrains updates to the pretrained column space, yielding a preconditioned optimization scheme that outperforms unconstrained Full FT at the same trainable parameter count. Building on this insight, we propose FuRA (Full-Rank Adaptation), an efficient full-rank adaptation framework based on a block tensor-train factorization W = LSR, where the large core L is fixed to the pretrained block-wise SVD basis, while only the compact core R and the block-wise singular values S are optimized. This design simultaneously provides full-rank spectral preconditioning, preserves full-rank update expressivity, and achieves parameter, memory, and step-time efficiency comparable to LoRA. FuRA consistently outperforms Full FT across multiple settings, including LLM fine-tuning (+1.37 on LLaMA-3-8B commonsense reasoning), LLM reinforcement learning for mathematical reasoning, and visual instruction tuning for VLMs. Furthermore, the 4-bit quantized variant, QFuRA, also surpasses QLoRA. Code is available at https://github.com/olokevin/FuRA-NIPS

</details>


### [131] [MedExpMem: Adapting Experience Memory for Differential Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22872)
*Qianhan Feng,Zhongzhen Huang,Yakun Zhu,Yannian Gu,Winnie Chiu Wing Chu,Xiaofan Zhang,Qi Dou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出经验记忆框架MedExpMem，使医学视觉语言模型能够像医生一样积累鉴别诊断经验，在不同模型上实现最高7.0%的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学VLM缺乏从临床实践中积累鉴别诊断经验的能力，参数的静态知识无法随诊断过程进化。

Method: 构建两阶段经验记忆框架：初始实践暴露知识缺口，反思式再诊断精化理解；将诊断失败经验组织为成对鉴别笔记，编码关键区分特征、决策规则和推理错误模式。

Result: 在覆盖11个亚专科的放射学基准上实现一致性准确率提升，最高达7.0%。

Conclusion: MedExpMem是一种有竞争力的方法，能够解决参数化学习无法覆盖的医学适应需求。

Abstract: Experienced physicians develop diagnostic expertise through clinical practice, acquiring not only disease knowledge but also the ability to differentiate confusable conditions. Current medical vision-language models (VLMs) lack this capability -- their parameters encode static knowledge that does not evolve across diagnostic encounters. We propose MedExpMem, an experience memory framework enabling VLM-based diagnostic agents to accumulate differential diagnosis expertise. Unlike retrieval-augmented generation, which retrieves encyclopedic disease descriptions, MedExpMem memorizes discriminative experience derived from the agent's own diagnostic failures and organizes them as pairwise differential notes encoding key discriminators, actionable decision rules and reasoning error patterns. The framework adopts a two-phase construction process mirroring physician learning: initial practice exposes knowledge gaps, and reflective re-diagnosis refines understanding. When encountering new cases, the agent retrieves experience memory to guide differential reasoning. We evaluate MedExpMem on a radiology benchmark spanning 11 subspecialties. Results demonstrate consistent accuracy improvements, maximum 7.0%, across diverse models and scales. Analytical experiments validate experience quality and robustness, demonstrating MedExpMem as a competitive method addresses medical adaptation needs beyond the reach of parameteric learning.

</details>


### [132] [When Do LLMs Reason? A Dynamical Systems View via Entropy Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22873)
*Wei Xia,Haoqing Wang,Zhi-Hong Deng,Yehui Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于熵动力学的轻量级推理路由框架EDRM，通过早期解码熵动态自适应选择推理策略，在15个基准和4个LLM上实现41-55%的token减少，同时保持或提升准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链式思维推理（CoT）在事实性和开放式任务中可能带来边际收益甚至负收益，同时大幅增加token消耗。现有方法将推理视为任务或模型的静态属性，忽略了推理生成过程中的动态状态变化，需要一种更高效、自适应的推理策略选择机制。

Method: 提出EDRM（熵动力学推理流形）框架：基于早期解码阶段的熵动态信号（熵单调减少表示受益于CoT，不稳定或增加表示不适用），将熵轨迹嵌入到紧凑且可解释的流形表示中，实现零样本部署和细粒度实例级自适应推理策略选择。

Result: 在15个基准和4种不同规模/架构的LLM上，EDRM在数据集级别实现41-55%的token减少并提升准确率（仅需50个校准样本）；在实例级别进一步将准确率提升高达4.7%，同时保持27-45%的token节省。

Conclusion: 推理应当有选择性地调用而非默认启用，熵驱动的解码控制是高效且自适应LLM推理的有效方法。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has become the default strategy for enhancing LLM capabilities, yet its application raises a fundamental question: when is explicit reasoning actually beneficial? Empirical evidence reveals a striking paradox: CoT often provides marginal or even negative gains on factual and open-ended tasks while multiplying token consumption. In this work, we show that LLM reasoning is not a static property of tasks or models, but a \emph{dynamic decoding state} that emerges during generation. Through systematic analysis, we find early-stage entropy dynamics provide a reliable signal of this state: tasks benefiting from CoT exhibit consistent entropy reduction, while others display unstable or increasing patterns. This behavior can be interpreted as a phase-transition-like shift from a high-entropy exploratory regime to a low-entropy structured reasoning regime. Based on these insights, we propose \textbf{EDRM} (Entropy Dynamics-based Reasoning Manifold), a lightweight and training-free routing framework that leverages early decoding entropy to adaptively select inference strategies. EDRM embeds entropy trajectories into a compact and interpretable manifold representation, enabling both zero-shot deployment and fine-grained instance-level adaptation. Across 15 benchmarks and 4 LLMs of varying scales and architectures, EDRM consistently outperforms static baselines. At the dataset level, EDRM achieves \textbf{41--55\%} token reduction while improving accuracy with as few as 50 calibration samples. At the instance level, it further improves accuracy by up to \textbf{4.7\%} while maintaining \textbf{27--45\%} token savings. These results suggest that reasoning should be invoked selectively rather than by default, and demonstrate the effectiveness of entropy-driven decoding control for efficient and adaptive LLM inference.

</details>


### [133] [WeCon: An Efficient Weight-Conditioned Neural Solver for Multi-Objective Combinatorial Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22876)
*Xuan Wu,Jinbiao Chen,Yang Li,Lijie Wen,Chunguo Wu,Yuanshu Li,Yubin Xiao,Chunyan Miao,You Zhou,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多目标组合优化问题现有神经求解器在权重条件建模和偏好优化中的局限性，提出了WeCon求解器，包含门控残差融合、残差融合模块和高效偏好优化策略，在保持与SOTA相当的超体积指标下推理时间减少40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（1）仅在解码时注入一次权重，限制了权重条件上下文建模；（2）主要在编码时注入权重，导致解码时权重信号稀释；（3）偏好优化依赖纯随机采样构造解对，产生信息量不足的样本，训练效率低。

Method: （1）编码器中设计含三个注意力块和门控残差融合块的编码层，促进实例特征与权重的和谐交互；（2）解码器中引入即插即用的残差融合块缓解权重信号稀释；（3）提出高效偏好优化（EPO），通过构造高质量解生成信息量更丰富的样本对对训练效果进行改进。

Result: 在四种MOCOP变体、不同问题规模和分布模式上，WeCon实现了与SOTA求解器POCCO-W相当的超体积值，同时推理时间减少40%。消融实验验证了所有设计的贡献。

Conclusion: WeCon通过改进权重注入时机和偏好优化样本质量，在保持求解质量的同时显著提升了推理效率，验证了各模块设计的有效性。

Abstract: Existing neural solvers for Multi-Objective Combinatorial Optimization Problems (MOCOPs) commonly adopt decomposition-based strategies that scalarize an MOCOP into multiple subproblems associated with distinct weight vectors. However, they either inject weights only once during decoding, limiting weight-conditioned context modeling, or primarily during encoding, causing weight-signal dilution during decoding. Moreover, preference optimization methods rely on purely random sampling to construct solution pairs for training solvers, which often produces less informative pairs and thus leads to low training effectiveness. To better address these limitations, we propose an efficient Weight-Conditioned neural solver (WeCon). Specifically, we design an encoder layer with three attention blocks and our proposed Gated Residual Fusion (GRF) block to facilitate harmonious interaction between instance features and weights, thereby generating informative weight-conditioned context. We further introduce a plug-and-play Residual Fusion (RF) block in the decoder to alleviate weight-signal dilution. Finally, we propose Efficient Preference Optimization (EPO), which constructs high-quality solutions, thereby generating more informative pairs to improve training effectiveness. Experiments on four MOCOP variants across different problem scales and distribution patterns demonstrate that WeCon achieves HyperVolume (HV) values comparable to SOTA solver POCCO-W, while reducing inference time by 40%. Ablation studies validate the contributions of all designs.

</details>


### [134] [Tensor Cache: Eviction-conditioned Associative Memory for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22884)
*Kabir Swain,Sijie Han,Daniel Karl I. Weidele,Mauro Martino,Antonio Torralba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tensor Cache是一种两层级KV缓存压缩方法，通过滑动窗口注意力（L1）与外积快速权重记忆（L2）结合，将窗口驱逐的KV对压缩为矩阵A，未来查询通过单次矩阵乘法读取，解决了滑动窗口丢弃长程证据的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归Transformer中KV缓存随上下文长度线性增长，滑动窗口缓存虽然限制内存但完全丢弃被驱逐的token，导致窗口外的相关证据不可访问。

Method: 提出双层级缓存：L1为滑动窗口softmax注意力保留近期token精确注意力；L2为固定大小的外积快速权重记忆，将窗口驱逐的KV对压缩为逐层矩阵A，利用线性注意力恒等式通过单次矩阵乘法读取。学习标量门控融合L1和L2输出，每头衰减率和写入率端到端训练。此外指出常见的分块均值训练捷径A←λA+η( k⊗ v)会每块引入C²-C个虚假跨token外积，并通过并行加权和扫描（等价于逐token写入，误差在float32精度内）消除该差距。

Result: 在系统扩展、可控关联回忆、长上下文语言建模和记忆容量诊断等任务上，Tensor Cache在记忆-质量前沿上优于有界状态基线。

Conclusion: Tensor Cache通过将滑动窗口驱逐的KV对压缩为外积快速权重记忆，有效改善了滑动窗口模型的内存-质量权衡。该工作还识别并修复了常见训练捷径中的虚假外积问题。

Abstract: Autoregressive Transformer KV caches grow linearly with context length; sliding-window caching bounds memory but discards evicted tokens entirely, so relevant evidence outside the window becomes inaccessible. We introduce \emph{Tensor Cache}, a two-level cache that pairs sliding-window softmax attention as a first-level cache (L1) with a fixed-size outer-product fast-weight memory as a second-level cache (L2) fed by KV pairs evicted from the window. Recent tokens remain in exact local attention; evicted pairs are compressed into a per-layer matrix $A$ and read by future queries through a single matrix multiplication, exploiting the linear-attention identity $q_t(k_i \otimes v_i)=\langle q_t,k_i\rangle v_i$. A learned scalar gate fuses the L1 and L2 outputs, and per-head decay and write-rate parameters are trained end-to-end. The outer-product memory and the read identity are well-known; our contribution is their use as an L2 cache fed exclusively by sliding-window evictions, plus identifying that the common chunked-mean training shortcut $A\!\leftarrow\!λA\!+\!η(\bar k\!\otimes\!\bar v)$ silently introduces $C^2{-}C$ spurious cross-token outer products per chunk, and closing the gap with a parallel weighted-sum scan equivalent to per-token writes within float32 epsilon. Across systems scaling, controlled associative recall, long-context language modeling, and memory-capacity diagnostics, Tensor Cache improves the memory--quality frontier over bounded-state baselines.

</details>


### [135] [Pointwise Metrics Mislead: An Evaluation Protocol for Multimodal Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22891)
*Mads H. Baattrup,Jörn Bach,Laurids Jeppe,Finn Labe,Alexander Grohsjean,Christian Schwanenberger,Peer Stelldinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出在科学逆问题重建中，点式指标（如RMSE、MAE）会因多模态后验分布而导致边际谱系统性压缩偏差，并提出包含CRPS、谱保真度诊断和覆盖校准三部分的评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学重建评估普遍使用点式指标（如RMSE、MAE），隐含假设误差越小重建越好。但本文证明，当逆问题后验分布为多模态时，该假设会导致结构性失败——点估计器会使边缘分布比真实分布更窄，压缩了科学测量依赖的尾部、模态等谱特征。

Method: 提出三部分评估协议：1）逐事件分布准确性用CRPS（连续排序概率评分）；2）总体边缘准确性用谱保真度诊断；3）不确定性可信度用覆盖校准。在合成基准（含解析后验）和粒子物理的真实多对一逆问题上进行验证。

Result: 点式指标与分布指标下的模型排序完全相反；校准进一步区分了CRPS无法区分的架构。评估协议而非模型本身决定了科学结论。

Conclusion: 传统的点式评估指标在逆问题中会误导科学结论，必须采用包含分布准确性、谱保真度和校准的三维评估协议，才能避免偏差并正确评估模型性能。

Abstract: Evaluation in scientific reconstruction is dominated by pointwise metrics - RMSE, MAE, per-event resolution - under the implicit assumption that lower error means better reconstruction. We show that this assumption fails structurally for inverse problems with multimodal posteriors. By the law of total variance, point estimators trained to minimize MSE or MAE produce a marginal spectrum strictly narrower than the truth whenever the posterior has nonzero width. The resulting bias is independent of architecture, training, and dataset size, and it compresses precisely the spectral features - tails, modes, shapes - that downstream scientific measurements rely on. We propose a three-part evaluation protocol where each step targets a failure mode the others miss: per-event distributional accuracy via CRPS, population-level marginal accuracy via a spectrum-fidelity diagnostic, and uncertainty trustworthiness via coverage-based calibration. On a synthetic benchmark with an analytic posterior and on a realistic many-to-one inverse problem from particle physics, model rankings reverse between pointwise and distributional metrics, and calibration further separates architectures indistinguishable under CRPS. The evaluation protocol, not the model, determines the scientific conclusion.

</details>


### [136] [From Residuals to Reasons: LLM-Guided Mechanism Inference from Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22897)
*Mohammad R. Rezaei,Rahul G. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MARICL框架，通过多智能体残差上下文学习，让LLM分析基础模型预测残差并生成显式修正项，在多个科学基准上提升预测性能并实现机制泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在科学应用中面临预测与理解兼顾的挑战：黑箱模型缺乏可解释性，现有方法只能回答'哪些特征重要'而无法描述特征交互，且无法与人类理解迭代优化。

Method: 采用多智能体框架，先让基础模型做预测，然后LLM分析师分析高残差样本，LLM理论家假设缺失结构生成修正公式，通过多轮文本梯度优化迭代改进。

Result: 在9个科学基准上MARICL均优于基础模型。在Cell-Free Protein数据集中，冻结的修正公式在相同试剂协议下92%情况提升预测，不同协议下系统失败，成功边界与生化机制一致而非批次数量。

Conclusion: MARICL框架能提取可迁移的机制性知识，修正项反映真实科学结构而非批次噪声，实现了超越特定批次的机制泛化。

Abstract: A persistent challenge in machine learning for scientific applications is jointly achieving prediction and understanding. Statistical models excel on structured data but operate as black boxes, while existing interpretability methods are largely inspective: they answer "which features matter?" but do not articulate how features interact or refine explanations iteratively alongside human understanding. Asking an LLM to predict the target directly forces it to search the entire output space; we instead anchor predictions with a base model and ask the LLM the narrower question of what that model is missing. We introduce Multi-Agent Residual In-Context Learning (MARICL), an agentic framework in which LLM agents analyze where a base-model fails, hypothesize missing structure from high-residual examples provided in context, and produce explicit correction terms refined through multi-turn textual gradient optimization. Across nine benchmarks spanning scientific, biomedical, socioeconomic, and synthetic settings, MARICL improves consistently over its base model on all datasets. To test whether these corrections reflect real structure or batch-specific noise, we freeze formulas learned on one experimental batch of the Cell-Free Protein dataset and apply them (with no retraining and no further LLM calls) to held-out batches. Within the same reagent protocol, the frozen formulas improve predictions in over 92% of cases; across a different protocol, they fail systematically. The success boundary aligns with the biochemistry, not the batch count; direct evidence of mechanistic generalization.

</details>


### [137] [FIRMA: FIbonacci Ring Model Aggregation for Privacy-preserving Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22898)
*Rachid Hedjam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FIRMA提出三种渐进式无服务器联邦学习协议，通过斐波那契加权环聚合、准确率门控邻居抑制和2-opt环排列，在28个配置中实现无服务器、永久私有分类头、环拓扑和非对称邻居加权，其中完整版\fibflpp在标签偏斜场景下最高超越FedAvg +20.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习协议面临结构性三难困境：服务器架构存在单点故障和梯度反演风险；去中心化环状方法对半诚实节点暴露分类头且使用均匀权重；个性化方法重新引入中心聚合。尚无协议同时实现无服务器、永久私有分类头、环拓扑和原则性非对称邻居加权。

Method: 提出FIRMA协议族，包含三个渐进增强版本：1) FibFL——基于斐波那契权重的无服务器环聚合，永久私有分类头；2) FibFLP——增加准确率门控邻居抑制；3) FibFLPP——引入2-opt环排列最大化相邻客户端类别多样性、全局环覆盖及余弦退火自保留校准，并推导收敛率界及四个支持命题。

Result: 在28个配置（4个基准×7种异构性）的系统实验中，FibFLPP在所有12个标签偏斜配置中超越FedAvg，CIFAR-10上K=1时峰值优势+20.7个百分点。在Dirichlet异构性下，FibFLPP在28个配置中的17个取得最高准确率，成为无服务器协议中的帕累托最优方法。

Conclusion: FIRMA协议族成功解决了联邦学习的结构性三难困境，通过斐波那契加权、自适应邻居抑制和全局环优化，在无服务器场景下实现了优于经典FedAvg的性能，尤其在标签偏斜和异构数据分布下优势显著。

Abstract: Federated learning protocols face a structural trilemma: canonical server-based aggregation~\cite{mcmahan2017} creates a single point of failure and gradient inversion risk; decentralised ring-gossip alternatives~\cite{hu2019segmented} expose classification heads to semi-honest peers via uninformed uniform weights; and personalised methods~\cite{collins2021exploiting} reintroduce central aggregation. No existing protocol simultaneously achieves server-free operation, permanently private heads, ring topology, and principled asymmetric neighbour weighting. We propose FIRMA (\textbf{FI}bonacci \textbf{R}ing \textbf{M}odel \textbf{A}ggregation), a family of three progressively enhanced federated learning protocols: 1) \fibfl\ establishes the foundation: server-free ring aggregation with Fibonacci-weighted neighbour blending and permanently private classification heads. 2) \fibflp\ augments this with accuracy-gated neighbour suppression, selectively down-weighting poorly-converged peers while preserving the Fibonacci directional bias. 3) \fibflpp, the full system, completes the family with a 2-opt ring permutation that maximises adjacent-client class diversity, global ring coverage via $K_g{=}\lceil N/2\rceil$ gossip passes, and cosine-annealed self-retention calibration. We establish a convergence rate bound and three supporting propositions governing normalisation, coverage, retention, and diversity optimality. Systematic experiments across 28 configurations -- four benchmarks crossed with seven heterogeneity regimes -- demonstrate that \fibflpp\ surpasses \fedavg\ in all 12 label-skew configurations, with a peak advantage of $+20.7$\,pp on CIFAR-10 at $K{=}1$. Under Dirichlet heterogeneity, \fibflpp\ is the Pareto-dominant method among all server-free protocols, achieving the highest accuracy in 17 of 28 configurations.

</details>


### [138] [Building a privacy-preserving Federated Recommender system for mobile devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22924)
*Aasheesh Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段联邦推荐系统，将（非敏感）偏好数据在云端处理与（敏感）移动上下文数据在设备端处理严格分离，仅传输模型更新/梯度，并在多数据集验证后以Kotlin Multiplatform形式提供跨平台生产级实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动设备上个性化推荐的传统做法需要将用户敏感数据集中到中央服务器，这违背了现代隐私期望和地域法规要求。

Method: 两阶段联邦推荐管道：第一阶段在云端基于非敏感的App上下文数据运行协同过滤模型生成候选短列表；第二阶段在设备端利用敏感的移动信号对候选进行重排序，仅模型更新/梯度离开设备。

Result: 在MovieLens、UCI Human Activity Recognition和私有试点数据集上验证了该方法的有效性，并交付了可部署于Android和iOS的Kotlin Multiplatform生产级库。

Conclusion: 通过非敏感与敏感数据的严格分离以及联邦学习机制，在保持推荐效果的同时显著提升了隐私保护水平，并提供了跨平台生产级实现。

Abstract: Serving personalized content on mobile devices has traditionally required pooling sensitive user data on centralized servers, a practice increasingly at odds with modern privacy expectations and geographical regulations. We present a two-stage federated recommendation system pipeline for mobile devices, built around a principled separation between non-sensitive user preference data and sensitive mobile context data that never leaves the device. The first stage runs a collaborative filtering model on non-sensitive app-context data in the cloud to generate a shortlist of relevant items. The second stage re-ranks these candidates on-device using sensitive mobile signals, with only model updates/gradients ever leaving the device. We validate the approach on MovieLens, UCI Human Activity Recognition, and a proprietary pilot dataset, and deliver a production-ready implementation as a Kotlin Multiplatform library deployable on Android and iOS.

</details>


### [139] [MARGIN: Runtime Confidence Calibration for Multi-Agent Foundation Model Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22949)
*Joss Armstrong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MARGIN，一种在线校准方法，通过实时学习每个智能体在不同置信度区间的校准因子，无需模型访问和重训练，在分布偏移下将校准误差降低3-6倍，并将多智能体选择的成对正确率从低于随机（45-56%）提升至70-89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准方法依赖智能体自我报告的置信度进行加权，但基础模型置信度在困难任务上存在系统性的错误校准且与准确性呈负相关。传统设计时校准方法（如温度缩放、Platt缩放、直方图分箱）因依赖固定的校正函数，在分布偏移下性能严重退化。

Method: MARGIN（Multi Agent Runtime Grading via Incremental Normalization）：一种在线校准方法，利用对称指数加权移动平均和贝叶斯收缩混合技术，从任务流中实时学习每个智能体在每个置信度区间的校准因子，无需模型访问、保留数据或重新训练。

Result: 在19个基础模型、8个基准测试和超过50,000个观察样本上，MARGIN在分布偏移下比最佳设计时基线方法降低3-6倍校准误差。在多智能体选择任务中，原始口头置信度在困难基准测试上的成对分辨率低于随机（45-56%），而MARGIN将其完全纠正至70-89%，在四个基准测试中的三个上超越了始终选择最佳模型的预言机。

Conclusion: MARGIN通过实时在线校准有效解决了基础模型置信度在分布偏移下的错误校准问题，显著提升了多智能体系统的决策可靠性。六个形式化命题分别描述了收敛性、跟踪速度和针对非策略智能体的对称更新的最优性，所有预测均通过实证验证。

Abstract: Foundation model agents increasingly operate in multi-agent deployments where a coordinator must decide which agent's response to trust. The standard approach weights agents by their self-reported confidence, but recent evidence shows that foundation model confidence is systematically mis-calibrated and, on hard tasks, inversely correlated with accuracy. Design-time calibration methods (temperature scaling, Platt scaling, histogram binning) cannot address this problem because they fit a fixed correction to held-out data and degrade under distribution shift.
  We present MARGIN (Multi Agent Runtime Grading via Incremental Normalization), an online calibration method that learns per-agent, per-confidence-band calibration factors from the task stream itself, requiring no model access, no held-out data, and no retraining. MARGIN uses symmetric exponentially weighted moving averages with Bayesian shrinkage blending, and has three hyperparameters with robust defaults. Across 19 foundation models, 8 benchmarks, and over 50,000 observations, MARGIN achieves 3-6x lower calibration error than the best design-time baseline under distribution shift. In multi-agent selection, raw verbalized confidence produces pairwise resolution worse than random (45-56%) on hard benchmarks. MARGIN corrects this completely, raising pairwise resolution to 70-89% and surpassing the always-best-model oracle on three of four benchmarks. Six formal propositions characterize convergence, tracking speed, and the optimality of symmetric updates for non-strategic agents, with all predictions illustrated empirically.

</details>


### [140] [FederatedRSF : Federated Random Survival Forests for Partially Overlapping Medical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22954)
*Maryam Moradpour,Jonas Harriehausen,Amirreza Aleyasin,Lion Philipp Wolf,Youngjun Park,Anne-Christin Hauschild*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FederatedRSF是一个Python包，通过聚合本地训练的生存树并仅重新分配特征兼容的树给各站点，实现了在特征空间异构（部分特征重叠）的多中心环境中进行联邦随机生存森林分析，无需共享原始数据。在GBSG2乳腺癌数据集上的实验表明，其性能与集中式训练相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多中心生存预测可提升鲁棒性与泛化性，但隐私法规和机构治理禁止跨中心共享患者级临床和基因组数据；此外，不同站点收集的协变量或测序panel不同导致特征空间仅部分重叠，进一步增加了部署难度。

Method: 提出FederatedRSF包，实现联邦随机生存森林：在各站点本地训练生存树，然后聚合模型，仅将特征兼容的树重新分发回各站点，从而在仅部分特征重叠的情况下进行推理，且不共享原始数据。

Result: 在scikit-survival包的GBSG2乳腺癌队列上，通过保留部分特征子集模拟客户端特征异质性，使用Harrell's C-Index在重复交叉验证和站点分割下评估。结果显示联邦模型的性能与集中式训练相当。

Conclusion: FederatedRSF能够在保护隐私和应对特征空间异构的条件下，实现与集中式训练可比的生存预测性能。

Abstract: Multi-center survival prediction can improve robustness and generalizability, yet privacy regulations and institutional governance often prevent pooling patient-level clinical and genomic data across institutions. In practice, deployment is further complicated by feature-space heterogeneity, in which sites collect different covariates or use different sequencing panels, resulting in only partially overlapping feature sets. We present FederatedRSF, a Python package that implements federated random survival forests, aggregating locally trained survival trees and redistributing only feature-compatible trees to each site, enabling inference with partial overlap without sharing raw data. We evaluate FederatedRSF on the GBSG2 breast cancer cohort distributed with the scikit-survival package, simulating feature heterogeneity across clients by withholding subsets of features, and assessing discrimination using Harrell's concordance index (C-Index) under repeated cross-validation and site-splits. The results demonstrated that the federated model can achieve performance comparable to that of the centralized training setting.

</details>


### [141] [Learned Relay Representations for Forward-Thinking Discrete Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22967)
*Benjamin Rozonoyer,Jacopo Minniti,Dhruvesh Patel,Neil Band,Avishek Joey Bose,Tim G. J. Rudner,Andrew McCallum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Learned Relay Representations (Relay)方法，通过在掩码扩散模型（MDM）的去噪步骤间传递潜在信息，避免每一步重算内部表示，在保持生成质量的同时将推理延迟降低32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MDM在序列生成时，每一步去噪都会丢弃前一步掩码位置上的丰富内部计算信息，迫使后续步骤重复计算，导致计算浪费和延迟增加。

Method: 设计一个可微分的每令牌通道，在MDM的前向传播之间传递潜在信息，并通过截断的BPTT训练该通道；该方法与块扩散、KV缓存等技术兼容。

Result: 在Sudoku规划任务上验证了设计选择；在Fast-dLLM v2（SOTA扩散语言模型）上，Relay在代码任务上超越标准监督微调，并将推理延迟降低最多32%。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型可以被显式训练以跨解码步骤前向传递潜在信息，从而推进性能-延迟帕累托前沿。

Abstract: When Masked Diffusion Models (MDMs) generate sequences through iterative refinement, the rich internal computation over masked positions is discarded, forcing every subsequent refinement step to recompute the valuable internal information stored as model representations. To avoid a hard reset between denoising rounds, we propose Learned Relay Representations (Relay), a method that allows MDMs to be forward-thinking when denoising by explicitly learning how to propagate latent information for the benefit of future denoising steps. Relay introduces a differentiable per-token channel that passes information between forward passes and is trained via truncated backpropagation through time (BPTT). We show that this framework can be scaled to state-of-the-art Diffusion Language Models (DLMs), and is seamlessly compatible with techniques like block diffusion and KV caching. We first provide a thorough justification of the design choices in Relay on a challenging Sudoku-based planning task. We then scale Relay to Fast-dLLM v2, a state-of-the-art DLM, outperforming standard supervised finetuning on coding tasks while reducing inference latency by up to 32%. Our empirical results demonstrate that state-of-the-art DLMs can be explicitly trained to relay latent information forward across decoding steps, advancing the performance-latency Pareto frontier. We provide code for all our experiments.

</details>


### [142] [A mathematical theory of balancing relational generalization and memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22972)
*Luke Cheng,Samuel Lippl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出传递性推理加例外（TIE）新任务，通过核岭回归理论分析和预训练语言模型实验，揭示学习系统如何在关系泛化与例外记忆间取得平衡，并指出表征几何对泛化成功的关键影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务范式不足以研究学习系统在掌握普适规律与记忆例外之间的平衡机制。

Method: 设计传递性推理加例外任务；用核岭回归对多种表征和任务参数进行理论分析；在预训练语言模型上进行微调验证。

Result: 模型能平衡关系泛化与例外记忆，但泛化成功高度依赖表征几何；理论预测的系统性错误在语言模型中得到验证。

Conclusion: 理论阐明了学习系统平衡泛化与记忆的机制及失败原因，强调了探索该能力的新任务范式的必要性。

Abstract: Humans, animals, and modern machine learning models exhibit impressive abilities to learn complex behaviors and generalize these behaviors to unseen situations. This ability requires us to learn rules and regularities that allow for such generalizations. At the same time, in most complex environments, any rule will have its exceptions. How do learning systems balance between learning general regularities and memorizing exceptions? We argue that a lack of task paradigms has hindered the study of this essential ability. To address this gap, we introduce a novel task, transitive inference with exceptions, that tests for relational generalization and memorization of an exception to the relational rule. We then analytically characterize the behavior of a simple, theoretically tractable model of neural network learning (kernel ridge regression) across a broad family of representations and task parameters. We find that these models can balance between relational generalization and memorization, but unlike for transitive inference without an exception, successful generalization is sensitive to the specific representational geometry. We explain why this task is more challenging mechanistically by drawing on our analytical theory. Finally, we validate our theoretical insights in pretrained language models that are finetuned on ordered relations, finding that these models successfully generalize according to the transitive rule, but also make the kinds of systematic mistakes predicted by our theory. Overall, our theory shows how learning systems can balance between relational generalization and memorization, explains how this can go wrong, and emphasizes the need for new task paradigms designed to probe this ability.

</details>


### [143] [Worse than Random: The Importance of a Baseline for Unsupervised Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22973)
*Muhammad Rajabinasab,Michael E. Houle,Oussama Chelly,Arthur Zimek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将随机特征选择作为无监督特征选择方法的评估基线，实证表明许多现有先进方法在性能和效率上均不如随机基线，并强调新方法必须与随机基线进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前无监督特征选择方法缺乏统一的评估基线，难以衡量新方法的实际贡献。

Method: 将随机特征选择作为基线，与现有多种无监督特征选择方法在性能和效率上进行对比。

Result: 许多最先进的无监督特征选择方法在性能和效率上均被随机特征选择超越。

Conclusion: 强调在开发无监督特征选择新方法时，必须将随机特征选择作为严格基线，以确保方法有实质性改进。

Abstract: Many novel unsupervised feature selection methods are proposed each year, yet their empirical evaluation is limited to supervised and unsupervised evaluation metrics computed on selected datasets, along with comparisons to existing methods. However, in the absence of an established evaluation baseline, it is difficult to determine the value added to the existing literature by each of these methods, and how effective their underlying approaches are. We propose using random feature selection as a baseline for evaluating the unsupervised feature selection methods. We empirically show that many of the state-of-the-art methods in unsupervised feature selection are outperformed by random feature selection in both performance and efficiency. Accordingly, we emphasize on the strict requirement of considering random feature selection as a baseline in the development process of novel unsupervised feature selection methods to ensure a consistent improvement over random feature selection.

</details>


### [144] [Test-Time Training Undermines Safety Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22984)
*Simone Antonelli,Sadegh Akhondzadeh,Aleksandar Bojchevski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了测试时训练（TTT）范式在提升模型适应能力的同时，引入了新的安全漏洞，使攻击者能够绕过安全过滤器，在LoRA等微调方法下攻击成功率（ASR@10）高达95%和93%，且漏洞可迁移至生产级微调API。作者提出了基于困惑度偏移的轻量级检测器作为初步防御，但指出根本解决方案需要动态对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究未关注TTT这一动态适应范式在推理阶段引入的安全风险。作者发现模型在测试时自适应调整参数的能力可被攻击者利用来绕过安全机制，这是一个尚未被系统研究的新攻击面。

Method: 提出了三种针对TTT的威胁模型（少样本、生成阶段等），通过LoRA等微调方法实施越狱攻击，并采用有效性感知评估（validity-aware evaluation）来纠正因过拟合导致的虚假攻击成功率。

Result: 在跨模型家族和规模的实验中，少样本和生成阶段威胁模型的平均ASR@10分别达到95%和93%。攻击可迁移至生产级微调API，且标准评估指标因TTT导致的过拟合输出而被虚高。

Conclusion: TTT暴露了新的攻击面，显著增强了现有攻击的有效性，并削弱了已有安全护栏。作为第一步防御，提出了基于私有有害保留集困惑度偏移检测的轻量级服务端检测器，但根本解决需要动态对齐机制。

Abstract: Test-Time Training (TTT) is an emerging paradigm that enables models to adapt their parameters during inference, improving performance on tasks such as few-shot learning, retrieval-augmented generation, and complex reasoning. However, this dynamic adaptation introduces new vulnerabilities that adversaries can exploit to jailbreak models. We identify three threat models for TTT and demonstrate how attackers can leverage them to bypass safety filters. Our results show that TTT can significantly increase the Attack Success Rate (ASR) and the ASR over 10 generation trials (ASR@10). For example, under LoRA, the few-shot and generation-phase threat models achieve an average ASR@10 of 95% and 93% respectively, across models from different families and scales. These vulnerabilities transfer to production fine-tuning APIs. We also show that TTT-induced overfitting can produce degenerate outputs that inflate ASR under standard judges, and propose a validity-aware evaluation to correct for this. Our findings suggest that TTT exposes a new attack surface, strengthens attacks, and undermines existing safety guardrails. As a first step toward defense, we propose a lightweight provider-side detector that flags TTT requests via the perplexity shift on a private harmful holdout, but robust deployment will ultimately require dynamic alignment.

</details>


### [145] [Smoothed Elicitation Complexity for Approximate $Γ$-calibration of Discrete Classification Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23017)
*Jessica Finocchiaro,Victor Ganson,Drona Khurana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次针对强可排序离散属性（如众数、排序）提供了近似性质校准的结果。通过将离散属性与Lipschitz连续性质关联，作者刻画了离散属性的近似校准保证，并构造了设计Lipschitz性质的算法，证明了这些性质可通过后处理恢复为原始离散属性，同时确定了其Lipschitz引发复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近似性质校准工作主要限于连续标量性质，但许多实际关注的属性（如众数、排序）是离散的。此外，多类校准的定义面临指数级复杂度爆炸问题，基于性质（property）的校准虽可降低复杂度，但缺乏离散属性的理论保证。

Method: 作者利用Lipschitz连续性质作为桥梁，将强可排序离散性质的近似校准问题转化为连续空间上的校准分析。通过构造特定算法来设计Lipschitz性质，并证明这些性质经后处理可还原为原始离散性质，从而推导出离散性质的近似校准边界。

Result: 首次获得了强可排序离散性质的近似校准理论保证；刻画了该类离散属性的Lipschitz引发复杂度；提供了构造对应Lipschitz性质的具体算法。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了离散属性近似校准的理论空白，为多类概率预测中离散性质（如众数预测、排序评估）的可信度验证提供了形式化基础。

Abstract: One prominent method of evaluating machine learning model trustworthiness is the notion of calibration. In the binary outcome setting, a probabilistic predictor is calibrated if outcomes are realized according to a model's distributional prediction, conditioned on this prediction. Straightforward extensions of binary calibration definitions to probabilistic multiclass classifiers suffer from an exponential complexity blowup as the space of predictions grows exponentially in the number of classes $n$. As a remedy, Noarov and Roth (2023) propose multiclass calibration with predictions that are properties of the outcome distribution, reducing complexity from growing in the number of classes $n$ to the dimension $d$ of the property, called its elicitation complexity. Previous work on approximate property calibration is generally limited to continuous scalar properties, despite many relevant properties of interest being discrete, like the mode or rankings. We characterize the approximate property calibration of discrete properties which are strongly orderable by using Lipschitz continuous properties as an intermediary. This work is the first to our knowledge to provide approximate calibration results for discrete properties. Along the way, we characterize the Lipschitz elicitation complexity of strongly orderable discrete properties by constructing algorithms for designing these Lipschitz properties, which we prove can be post-processed to obtain the original discrete property.

</details>


### [146] [World Machine: Towards Generative World Modeling for Time-Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23025)
*Elton Cardoso do Nascimento,Alexandre da Silva Simões,Esther Luna Colombini,Ricardo Ribeiro Gudwin,Paula Dornhofer Paro Costa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: World Machine是一种基于Transformer的时间序列生成式世界模型架构，通过引入潜在状态来适应不同观测数据量和上下文，相比传统Transformer（计算和内存成本随上下文二次方增长）具有优势。在合成数据集Toy1D上的实验验证了该方法的可行性，展示了传统Transformer不具备的能力，并突出了训练协议各组成部分的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer在处理变长上下文时计算和内存成本呈二次方增长，难以高效适应不同观测数据量和上下文。世界模型作为一种生成式AI的新范式，追求对环境的结构化、可泛化的预测和可控模拟，因此需要一种更高效的架构来实现这一目标。

Method: 提出World Machine架构，基于Transformer并引入潜在状态（latent states），使其能够适应不同数量的观测数据和上下文长度，从而克服标准Transformer的二次方复杂度问题。

Result: 在合成的Toy1D数据集上的实验验证了World Machine的可行性，证明了其具备传统Transformer所不具备的能力（如对可变上下文的适应性），并通过消融实验明确了训练协议中每个组件的贡献。

Conclusion: World Machine通过潜在状态设计在时间序列建模中实现了对可变上下文的高效适应，为生成式世界模型在时间序列领域的应用提供了可行的方案，未来可推广至更复杂的现实场景。

Abstract: World models represent a paradigm shift in generative AI, pursuing predictive understanding and controllable simulation of environments in a structured and generalizable way. We present World Machine, a generative world-modeling architecture for time series. It is a transformer-based architecture with latent states that enables adaptation to different amounts of observed data and contexts. This shows an improvement over traditional transformers, which have a computational and memory cost that scales quadratically with the context. Experiments on a proposed synthetic dataset, Toy1D, validate the approach's feasibility, demonstrate capabilities not found in conventional transformers, and highlight the contributions of each component of the training protocol.

</details>


### [147] [RADAR: Relative Angular Divergence Across Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23028)
*Xavier Cadet,Mateusz Nowak,Peter Chin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RADAR指标，通过测量层间表示轨迹的几何对齐和距离变化来估计基础模型的跨域迁移性，在视觉和文本基准上表现竞争力强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据收集困难且扩充数据集可能引发负迁移，需要无需标注的迁移性估计方法。

Method: 分析基础模型逐层表示的几何演化——计算层间位移轨迹的角度对齐和相对距离变化，比较域内与跨域动力学经验分布，通过分布散度量化迁移性。

Result: 在跨语言情感分类和跨域图像分类等多个模态场景中，相比现有迁移性指标具有竞争力，尤其在域过渡平滑或清晰分离时表现突出。

Conclusion: 迁移性估计的有效性取决于模型内部表示空间的几何结构，不同模态偏好不同的拓扑公式。

Abstract: Machine learning methods rely on data. However, gathering suitable data can be challenging due to availability constraints, cost, or the need for domain expertise. Expanding datasets with additional sources is a common response to limited data, yet this practice does not always improve downstream performance and can sometimes lead to a loss of performance, known as negative transfer. We propose RADAR, a simple, geometrically grounded metric for estimating cross-domain transferability in foundation models. RADAR analyzes the layer-wise evolution of representations by measuring angular alignments and relative changes in distance along layer-to-layer displacement trajectories, and by comparing empirical distributions of within-domain and cross-domain dynamics. We hypothesize that domain transferability is related to the divergence between these trajectory distributions. We evaluate the metric across multiple modalities, including cross-lingual sentiment classification with text embedding models and cross-domain image classification with foundation vision models. Across several settings, RADAR provides competitive predictive performance relative to existing transferability metrics on several vision and text benchmarks, with particularly strong results when domain transitions are smooth or cleanly separated. Our ablations further suggest that the effectiveness of transferability estimation depends on the geometry of the model's internal representation space, with different modalities favoring different topological formulations.

</details>


### [148] [Uncovering the Latent Potential of Deep Intermediate Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23033)
*Arnesh Batra,Arush Gumber,Aniket Khandelwal,Jashn Khemani,Anubha Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LOES方法，通过逐层最优嵌入选择发现任务判别性子空间，并配合GeoReg正则化损失稳定微调时的表征几何结构，证明深层模型中逐层嵌入几何对知识迁移至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅使用最后一层或浅层混合表示，忽略了任务相关信息在层间非单调分布的特性，导致无法充分利用深层模型各层中编码的判别性结构。

Method: 提出LOES（逐层最优嵌入选择）光谱方法，通过正交性和各向同性约束最小化残差误差来识别任务判别性子空间；并提出GeoReg（几何正则化损失），通过在微调时施加单纯形结构来稳定类别流形的表征几何。

Result: 在多种架构、深度、模态和数据规模下，LOES一致优于标准基线，且优势随模型深度增加而扩大；同时揭示了语义因子在层间的分布，支持跨语言和跨模态可解释性分析。

Conclusion: 逐层嵌入几何不是偶然现象，而是深度模型表示和迁移知识的核心机制。

Abstract: Foundational Models pretrained on huge amount of data learn representations that evolve across depth, forming a hierarchy of embeddings with distinct semantic content and geometric structure. Contrary to the widespread practice of using only the final layer or shallow mixtures, we show that task-relevant information is distributed non-monotonically across layers and cannot be recovered by naïve aggregation. Through a geometric and empirical study across multiple modalities, we show that effective transfer depends on identifying which layers encode task-discriminative structure and how their embeddings are geometrically organized.
