As a result, progress in this area has been constrained by the lack of large-scale, real-world, publicly available pre-training data. We introduce BBO-Pile, the first open-source dataset comprising over 500K optimization trajectories evaluated across 3095 different black-boxes for different optimizers, which represents by far the largest public dataset for this task. Using this dataset, we train a family of foundation models at multiple scales, ranging from 2M to 80M parameters and from 200M to 2B training tokens, and study their scaling behavior with respect to compute. Our results demonstrate that large-scale pre-training is a viable and effective approach to imitate black-box optimization methods, paving the way for future research in this direction.

</details>


### [186] [Hinge Regression Trees and HRT-Boost: Newton-Optimized Oblique Learning for Compact Tabular Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23422)
*Hongyi Li,Jun Xu,Hong Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了铰链回归树（HRT）框架，将斜分叉转化为非线性最小二乘问题，并基于牛顿法优化；进一步提出HRT-Boost集成方法，在理论和实验上证明了其逼近能力与性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量斜决策树的学习因分裂优化的离散性和非凸性而具有挑战性，现有方法难以同时保证优化效率与理论保证。

Method: 将每个斜分裂重新表述为基于两个线性预测器的最大/最小包络（类似ReLU）的非线性最小二乘问题；节点级优化解释为带回溯线搜索的阻尼牛顿法，并证明目标单调递减；集成扩展HRT-Boost结合节点级牛顿更新与逐阶段函数梯度下降。

Result: 理论上证明了HRT是通用逼近器且具有显式O(δ²)逼近率；HRT-Boost在平方损失下具有逐阶段经验风险降低保证。实验表明HRT优于单树基线，HRT-Boost优于强集成基线且模型更紧凑。

Conclusion: HRT框架有效解决了斜决策树的学习难题，提供了理论保证和实际性能优势，HRT-Boost进一步增强了集成学习的效果与模型效率。

Abstract: Learning high-quality oblique decision trees remains a significant challenge due to the discrete and non-convex nature of split optimization. We present the Hinge Regression Tree (HRT) framework, which reframes each oblique split as a nonlinear least-squares problem over two linear predictors whose max/min envelope induces ReLU-like representation capacity. We show that the resulting node-level optimization can be interpreted as a damped Newton method, and we establish the monotonic decrease of the node objective for its backtracking line-search variant. We establish, theoretically, that HRT is a universal approximator with an explicit $O(δ^2)$ approximation rate. Building upon this base learner, we propose HRT-Boost, a mathematically synergistic ensemble extension that couples node-level Newton updates with stage-wise functional gradient descent. We show that this ensemble construction admits a stage-wise empirical risk reduction guarantee under the squared loss. Empirical evaluations on synthetic and real-world benchmarks show that HRT is highly competitive with established single-tree baselines, and HRT-Boost compares favorably with strong ensemble baselines and often yields substantially more compact models. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/Hongyi-Li-sz/HRT-Boost.

</details>


### [187] [Onsager-Machlup Posterior Transport for Deep Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23434)
*Jian Xu,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的深度高斯过程推断方法OM-Path，通过后验传输和Onsager-Machlup路径正则化，将推断转化为学习确定性采样器，在大型UCI数据集上显著优于现有方法DBVI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度高斯过程推断方法（如DSVI、DBVI等）在处理诱导变量时存在计算瓶颈——显式密度拟合的ELBO方法或MCMC采样方法效率有限，需要更高效的推断范式。

Method: 将DGP推断重新框架为后验传输问题：学习一个确定性采样器将可处理的参考测度映射到后验相关的诱导变量，通过Doob桥参考扩散的路径先验进行正则化，使用概率流ODE和Onsager-Machlup作用量作为路径正则化器。

Result: 在7个UCI回归基准上，OM-Path在两个最大数据集（power和protein）上获得统计显著的性能提升，在中等数据集上持平，在小噪声数据集上不如DBVI；严格ELBO变体在所有指标上均未超越DBVI。

Conclusion: 在相关路径空间中，降低路径目标的方差比精确密度追踪更重要；OM-Path通过有限ε训练目标（与Doob桥后验的MAP路径等价）实现了更高效的推断。

Abstract: Approximate inference over inducing variables is the central computational bottleneck of Deep Gaussian Processes (DGPs). Existing methods either fit an explicit density $q_φ(\bU)$ by an ELBO (DSVI, IPVI, DDVI, DBVI) or sample by MCMC (SGHMC). We instead frame DGP inference as \emph{posterior transport}: learn a deterministic sampler that maps a tractable reference measure to posterior-relevant inducing variables, regularised by a path prior derived from the Doob-bridged reference diffusion. Our realisation, \textbf{OM-Path} (formally FBVI-bridge-Path), uses Song's probability-flow ODE applied to DBVI's Doob-bridged forward SDE; the reference drift is closed-form from the bridge marginal coefficients (no score matching) and the path regulariser is the \textbf{Onsager--Machlup action}. At the finite-$ε$ value used at training, the objective is the negative log unnormalised density of a tempered Doob-bridge path posterior, and Theorem 1 identifies it with the same posterior's small-noise MAP path via the Freidlin--Wentzell LDP. Two strict path-space ELBO variants on the same bridge backbone (FFJORD log-det; OM-regularised CNF) are derived as ablations. Under a matched-seed paired Wilcoxon test against DBVI on seven UCI regression benchmarks, OM-Path delivers statistically significant wins on the two largest datasets (\textit{power}: $p\!=\!0.014$, NLL $\mathbf{0.012}$ matching the DSVI baseline of $0.017$; \textit{protein}: $p\!=\!0.002$, RMSE $\mathbf{0.716}$ vs.\ $0.764$, NLL $\mathbf{1.086}$ vs.\ $1.149$), statistical ties on \textit{yacht} / \textit{qsar}, and concedes \textit{boston} / \textit{energy} / \textit{concrete} to DBVI on small-$N$ noisy data. The strict-ELBO variants do not clear DBVI on any UCI metric: in this regime, reducing the variance of the path objective dominates exact-density tracking.

</details>


### [188] [Commutator-Induced Uncertainty in VAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23449)
*Tahereh Dehdarirad,Michael Felsberg,Gabriel Eilertsen,Ziliang Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Lie Group VAE框架，通过两阶段训练（先无约束诊断非交换性，再施加形变稳定性约束）来显式建模潜在空间中的非交换结构，在多个基准上提升了重建质量和潜在遍历的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准VAE和对称性感知VAE（如通过代数正则化强制交换性）无法恰当处理数据中固有的非交换结构，导致重建行为与潜在非交换性之间存在尺度不匹配。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段无约束训练，通过有限Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff偏差测量代数非交换性，并用重建顺序交换测试测量解码器顺序敏感性；第二阶段引入带有数据驱动校准常数的形变稳定性约束，使解码器灵敏度与代数非交换性对齐。

Result: 在dSprites、3DShapes、3DCars和CelebA上对比beta-VAE、CLG-VAE、CFASL等基线，合成基准上重建质量提升且解码器行为更符合潜在非交换结构；CelebA上重建更忠实、潜在遍历更因子特定，且展现出有意义的顺序依赖交互。

Conclusion: 显式诊断和对齐非交换性能够提升VAE对非交换结构的建模能力，优于强制交换性的对称性方法。该框架为实现更忠实、结构感知的潜在表示提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Variational autoencoders (VAEs) often struggle to represent non-commutative structure in learned latent spaces. Symmetry-aware VAEs commonly address this issue by enforcing commutativity through algebraic regularization, which is appropriate for commutative transformation groups but can suppress meaningful non-commutative structure when it is intrinsic to the data. We argue that non-commutativity should instead be explicitly diagnosed and reflected in reconstruction behavior. We introduce a Lie Group VAE framework that combines geometric and algebraic perspectives on uncertainty while separating discrete generative factors from continuous geometric transformations. In a first phase, the model is trained without structural constraints while algebraic non-commutativity is measured through finite Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff deviations and decoder order sensitivity is measured through reconstruction order-swap tests. These diagnostics reveal a scale mismatch between latent non-commutativity and reconstruction behavior under unconstrained training. In a second phase, we introduce a deformation-stability constraint with a data-driven calibration constant that aligns decoder sensitivity with algebraic non-commutativity. We evaluate the framework on dSprites, 3DShapes, 3DCars, and CelebA against generic and symmetry-aware baselines, including beta-VAE, CLG-VAE, and CFASL. Across synthetic benchmarks, the method improves reconstruction quality and yields decoder-level behavior more consistent with latent non-commutative structure. Qualitative analyses show clearer order-dependent latent compositions and more stable reconstructions. On CelebA, the model yields more faithful reconstructions and factor-specific latent traversals than CFASL, while also exhibiting meaningful order-dependent interactions between learned latent directions.

</details>


### [189] [Class-Dependent Hybrid Data Augmentation for Multiclass Migraine Classification under Severe Class Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23453)
*Elvin Somón,Miguel A. Gutiérrez-Naranjo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过纠正数据泄露和指标偏差，重新评估了先前的偏头痛分类研究，提出了临床驱动的亚型合并、基于类样本量的混合增强策略以及保真度不对称概念。在400名患者七种亚型的数据集上，新框架在平均宏F1上优于单独增强方法（0.862 vs 0.836/0.815/0.801），FT-Transformer在比例增强下达到0.914峰值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏头痛分类研究存在数据泄露和指标偏差导致性能虚高的问题，需要重新评估并开发更稳健的分类方法。

Method: 提出三种创新：临床驱动的偏头痛亚型合并（遵循ICHD-3 §1.2.3）、基于类样本量的混合增强策略、以及保真度不对称概念（用比例约束增长替代完全类别平衡）。采用两阶段协议在400患者七亚型数据集上评估。

Result: 纠正方法缺陷后基线宏F1降至0.71；新框架在八个分类器平均宏F1上达到0.862（优于高斯Copula 0.836、CTGAN 0.815和无增强0.801）；FT-Transformer在比例增强下取得峰值0.914。

Conclusion: 临床驱动的类别合并贡献了大部分绝对提升，框架的主要可测量贡献是增强了跨分类器的平均鲁棒性，表明问题重新定义（数据重组）比增强技术本身更为关键。

Abstract: We conducted a reproducibility-oriented re-evaluation of prior migraine classification studies, correcting for data leakage and metric bias. We then introduced (i) a clinically motivated aggregation of two hemiplegic subtypes following ICHD-3 §1.2.3, (ii) a class-dependent hybrid augmentation strategy that assigns generation methods based on per-class sample size, and (iii) the concept of fidelity asymmetry, motivating proportionally constrained growth as an alternative to full class balance. Experiments were performed on a dataset of 400 patients across seven migraine subtypes under a two-stage protocol, including the six-class configuration described above. Models were evaluated using stratified 5-fold cross-validation with macro-averaged F1 as the primary metric.
  Correcting methodological flaws reduces previously inflated performance estimates, with the corrected macro-F1 baseline standing at 0.71. The proposed framework consistently outperformed individual augmenters in macro-F1 averaged across the eight evaluated classifiers (0.862 vs. 0.836 for Gaussian Copula, 0.815 for CTGAN, and 0.801 for the no-augmentation baseline), and achieved its peak result of 0.914 with FT-Transformer under proportional augmentation. The no-augmentation FT-Transformer baseline (0.896) shows that, at the per-classifier ceiling, clinically motivated class aggregation accounts for most of the absolute improvement; the framework's principal measurable contribution is the gain in average robustness across classifiers, highlighting the dominant role of problem formulation.

</details>


### [190] [Automated Random Embedding for Practical Bayesian Optimization with Unknown Effective Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23473)
*Hong Qian,Xiang Shu,Xiang Xia,Xuhui Liu,Yangde Fu,Bei Liang,Huibin Wang,Liang Dou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出动态共享嵌入贝叶斯优化（DSEBO），通过自适应调整子空间维度并共享查询解，解决高维黑箱函数优化中有效维度未知的难题，理论证明其遗憾界，实验显示在优化遗憾与时间上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯优化受维度灾难困扰，随机嵌入虽能降维但需预先知晓任务的有效维度，而实际中有效维度往往未知；现有固定子空间维度或试错法耗费资源且性能不佳。

Method: 提出DSEBO：从低维子空间开始优化，当当前子空间解初步收敛时自动切换到更高维子空间；通过不同子空间解的质量动态决定下一子空间维度，并将已查询的解共享给新子空间以改善初始化。

Result: 理论上推导了DSEBO的遗憾界，证明其能更好平衡逼近误差与优化误差；在多种维度函数及未知有效维度的实际任务上，相比SOTA方法显著降低了优化遗憾并节省了时间。

Conclusion: DSEBO通过跨不同子空间交替优化，有效解决了有效维度未知下的高维贝叶斯优化问题，在性能与效率上均取得明显提升。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization is widely employed for optimizing complex black-box functions but struggles with the curse of dimensionality. Random embedding, as a dimension reduction strategy, simplifies tasks that possess the effective dimension by optimizing within a low-dimensional subspace. However, determining the effective dimension of a task in advance remains a significant challenge, which influences the selection of the subspace dimensionality and the optimization performance. Traditional methods use fixed subspace dimensions provided by experts or rely on trial and error to estimate subspace dimensions with resources consumed. To this end, this paper proposes an automated random embedding for high-dimensional Bayesian optimization with unknown effective dimension, called Dynamic Shared Embedding Bayesian Optimization (DSEBO). DSEBO starts with a low dimension and switches to a higher subspace if the solutions in the current subspace show preliminary convergence. DSEBO dynamically determines the dimension of the next subspace based on the quality of the solutions in different subspaces and shares the queried solutions with the new subspace for a better initialization. Theoretically, we derive a regret bound for DSEBO and demonstrate that DSEBO can better balance approximation and optimization errors. Extensive experiments on functions with dimensionality of varying magnitudes and real-world tasks with unknown effective dimensions reveal that, compared with state-of-the-art methods, alternating optimization across different subspaces results in significant improvements in high-dimensional optimization, both in terms of optimization regret and time.

</details>


### [191] [Precise: SDE-Consistent Stochastic Sampling for RL Post-Training of Flow-Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23522)
*Jade Zou,Tao Huang,Weijie Kong,Junzhe Li,Yue Wu,Qi Tian,Jiangfeng Xiong,Jianwei Zhang,Liefeng Bo,Zhao Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Precise的新型随机采样器，用于解决扩散和流匹配生成器中强化学习时的探索-稳定性平衡问题。通过冻结干净潜在后验均值的近似方法，Precise保持了去噪轨迹的SDE一致性，在RL奖励优化中实现了更快速、更稳定的收敛，并在PickScore、HPSv2.1等对齐指标上达到最优，同时训练时间减少13.1%-53.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在将在线强化学习应用于流匹配生成器时，必须将确定性ODE采样轨迹转换为随机SDE策略。然而，现有随机采样器在设计上存在两个关键问题：一是探索与去噪稳定性之间存在固有矛盾，二是在RL常用的小步数条件下，现有采样器无法忠实离散化SDE，要么引入过多离散噪声，要么依赖不保证收敛到数据分布的启发式规则。

Method: 本文首先分析了去噪过程中探索与稳定性之间的权衡关系，推导出能够平衡两者的SDE调度方案。针对离散化挑战，通过玩具示例揭示了现有采样器偏离流匹配过程的根本原因。在此基础上提出Precise采样器，其核心创新在于通过冻结干净潜在后验均值（clean-latent posterior mean）的新型近似方法，使去噪轨迹保持SDE一致性，从而解决了标准采样器中的过量噪声问题。

Result: 大量实验表明，Precise采样器在强化学习奖励优化中实现了显著更快、更稳定的收敛。在PickScore、HPSv2.1等对齐指标上达到了最先进水平，同时相比之前的最优采样器，训练时间减少了13.1%-53.2%。

Conclusion: 随机采样器的设计是影响流匹配生成器在线RL奖励优化性能的关键因素。通过合理设计SDE调度和离散化策略，Precise采样器有效平衡了探索与稳定性，证明了在RL训练中保持SDE一致性对提升对齐质量和训练效率的重要性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become an effective way to improve prompt alignment and perceptual quality in diffusion and flow-matching generators. A critical step for applying online RL to flow matching is turning the deterministic sampling trajectory into a stochastic policy, typically by replacing the reverse-time Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) with a Stochastic Differential Equation (SDE). The stochastic sampler, controlling the exploration behavior and denoising dynamics, is thus part of the policy, and its design can significantly affect the reward optimization performance. We break down the sampler design into two interdependent components: choosing the right amount of stochastic exploration, and discretizing the resulting SDE faithfully at the small step counts used in RL. To address the first component, we analyze the inherent tension between exploration and stability in denoising and derive an SDE schedule that balances the two. Turning to the discretization challenge, we use a toy example to show that existing samplers can deviate from the flow-matching process, either by introducing excessive discretization noise or by relying on heuristic rules that do not guarantee convergence to the data distribution. To address these issues, we propose Precise, a new stochastic sampler that balances effective exploration with stability. Crucially, Precise keeps the denoising trajectory SDE-consistent through a novel approximation that freezes the clean-latent posterior mean, resolving the excess noise issue in standard samplers. Extensive experiments demonstrate that this formulation leads to significantly faster and more stable reward optimization via reinforcement learning, achieving state-of-the-art alignment scores (e.g., PickScore, HPSv2.1) while requiring 13.1-53.2% less wall-clock training time to match the best in-domain performance of prior samplers.

</details>


### [192] [VACE: Learning Geometrically Structured Representations for Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23504)
*Alberto D. Cencillo,Leonardo Concepción,Isaac Triguero,Julián Luengo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VACE通过速度一致性自监督学习，将多元时间序列中的正常行为表征为嵌入空间中紧凑且方向一致的区域，结合马氏距离和速度银行方向得分实现异常检测，在TSB-AD-M基准上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比自监督方法通过配对采样启发式间接塑造嵌入空间，无法显式控制正常表征的紧凑性和方向性，导致距离评分缺乏几何结构保证。

Method: 提出VACE方法：1）采用无负样本、无合成异常的速度一致性目标训练通道感知编码器，使正常轨迹在局部平滑且方向对齐；2）测试时结合马氏位置得分和速度银行方向得分（相乘），同时检测分布偏移和动态异常。

Result: 在TSB-AD-M基准上以显著更低的计算开销超越更复杂的现有方法，达到最优性能。

Conclusion: VACE验证了通过显式约束嵌入空间几何结构（紧凑性+方向一致性）可有效提升多元时间序列异常检测性能，且无需负样本或合成异常，方法简洁高效。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in multivariate time series is a critical task across a wide range of real-world applications, where abnormal behaviour is rare, labels are unavailable, and the cost of a miss is high. The central challenge is learning a characterisation of normality precise enough to flag deviations. Representation self-supervised learning, typically through contrastive approaches, addresses this by embedding temporal patches into a latent space where normality occupies a well-defined region, with anomalies detected by geometric deviation. However, contrastive approaches shape this space indirectly through pair-sampling heuristics, providing no explicit control over the geometric structure that distance-based scoring requires. This means how tightly normal representations are grouped, and whether distances are directionally meaningful. We present VACE (Velocity-Aligned Channel Embeddings), a self-supervised anomaly detection method that represents normality as a compact, directionally coherent region in the embedding space. To this end, VACE trains a channel-aware encoder through a velocity-consistency objective, with no negatives and no synthetic anomalies, so that normal trajectories are locally smooth and aligned. At test time, a Mahalanobis positional score and a velocity-bank directional score are combined multiplicatively, flagging points that are simultaneously off-distribution and dynamically atypical. Despite its simplicity, VACE achieves state-of-the-art performance on TSB-AD-M under rigorous evaluation, significantly outperforming more complex methods trained on substantially larger budgets.

</details>


### [193] [Cost-Effective Model Evaluation with Meta-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23595)
*Trinh Pham,Viet Huynh,Hongzhi Yin,Quoc Viet Hung Nguyen,Thanh Tam Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MetaEvaluator，一种基于元学习的模型无关评估框架，可在无标注数据上高效评估新模型，显著降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型评估依赖昂贵标注、重复微调或狭窄假设，难以跨模型族迁移，且新模型在未见无标签数据上的可靠性验证变得日益困难。

Method: 通过元学习在参考模型池上获得可迁移初始化，对未见模型进行无标签快速评估，无需逐模型重新训练。

Result: 大量实验表明MetaEvaluator能够以远低于传统方法的成本生成稳定准确的性能估计。

Conclusion: MetaEvaluator是首个能够在完全无标签数据集上评估新模型的模型无关框架，使可扩展的无标签基准评估变得实用。

Abstract: The rapid growth of machine learning has produced an ever-expanding ecosystem of models, making it increasingly challenging to verify the reliability of newly released models on unseen, unlabeled data. Conventional evaluation pipelines depend on expensive annotation, repeated fine-tuning, or narrow assumptions that fail to transfer across model families. We present MetaEvaluator, a cost-effective, model-agnostic framework for rapid, label-free assessment of unseen models spanning diverse architectures and modalities. MetaEvaluator leverages meta-learning over a pool of reference models to obtain a transferable initialization, enabling accurate evaluation of new models while amortizing cost across the pool and removing the need for per-model retraining. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first model-agnostic framework capable of evaluating new models on entirely unlabeled datasets. Extensive experiments show that MetaEvaluator produces stable and accurate performance estimates at substantially reduced cost compared to conventional approaches, making scalable benchmarking of emerging models on unlabeled data practical.

</details>


### [194] [Debiased Negative Mining Improves Out-of-distribution Detection with Pre-trained Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23797)
*Bo Peng,Jie Lu,Guangquan Zhang,Zhen Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于去偏负例挖掘的OOD检测方法，通过理论框架校正负标签采样偏差，利用蒙特卡洛采样基于ID标签和未标注野生语料数据间接近似负标签分布，在多种OOD检测设置下取得了最先进的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM-based OOD检测方法依赖启发式规则从未标注野生语料中挖掘负标签，存在严重的假阴性问题（即误将真实负标签当作正标签或漏掉重要负标签），未能充分发挥VLM的OOD检测能力。

Method: 构建了一个理论框架来校正负标签的采样偏差，通过间接近似负标签分布，将去偏负挖掘自然地转化为基于ID标签和未标注野生语料数据的蒙特卡洛采样。

Result: 在多种OOD检测设置下进行了大量实验，实证表明该方法建立了新的最先进水平。

Conclusion: 通过理论驱动的去偏负挖掘方法，有效解决了负标签采样中的假阴性问题，充分发挥了预训练VLM在OOD检测中的潜力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Aiming at identifying unexpected inputs from unknown classes, out-of-distribution (OOD) detection has emerged as a pivotal approach to enhancing the reliability of machine learning models. This paper focuses on the burgeoning paradigm of post-hoc OOD detection with pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs), where a popular pipeline is to detect OOD inputs by examining their affinities between ID labels and negative labels, i.e., those semantically different from ID labels. Due to the unavailability of target OOD labels, existing works predominantly rely on heuristic rules to mine negative labels from unlabeled wild corpus data. Despite the empirical success, we argue that the power of VLM-based OOD detection has yet to be fully unleashed since the notorious false negative problem is far from addressed in the literature. With this motivation, we are interested in addressing the challenge of mining true negative labels for OOD scoring. To this end, we develop a theoretical framework for correcting the sampling bias of negatives labels by indirectly approximating the distribution of negative labels. Perhaps surprisingly, we show that the debiased negative mining can be naturally converted into Monte-Carlo sampling based on ID labels and the unlabeled wild corpus data. Extensive experiments empirically manifest that our method establishes a new state-of-the-art in a variety of OOD detection setups. Code is publicly available at \href{https://github.com/60pen9/Debiased-Negative-Mining-Improves-OOD-Detection-with-Pre-trained-VLMs}{\textcolor{red}{here}}.

</details>


### [195] [Leveraging Foundation Models for Causal Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23861)
*Aneesh Komanduri,Xintao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 引入FM-CGM框架，利用预训练基础模型（大语言推理模型+文本到图像扩散模型）实现零样本因果发现、干预和反事实生成，并提出因果语义引导（CSG）机制确保语义干预正确传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在生成模型训练中整合因果约束，但缺乏统一框架来利用预训练基础模型的零样本推理能力，需要一种模块化方法实现端到端视觉因果推理。

Method: 提出FM-CGM框架，包含概念提取器、概念操控器和反事实生成器三个核心组件；利用大语言推理模型进行因果推断，文本到图像扩散模型进行生成；开发基于交叉注意力的因果语义引导（CSG）机制，确保语义干预传播到子概念同时保留不变区域。

Result: 该方法能够识别合理的因果结构，并适用于生成忠实（faithful）的反事实图像。

Conclusion: FM-CGM提供了一种利用预训练基础模型实现零样本视觉因果推理的有效框架，CSG机制保证了因果干预的语义一致性。该工作发表于ICLR 2025。

Abstract: Causal generative modeling is essential for developing reliable and transparent AI systems capable of counterfactual reasoning. While existing approaches focus on integrating causal constraints during the training of generative models, they often lack a unified framework to leverage the zero-shot reasoning capabilities of pretrained foundation models. We introduce FM-CGM, a modular framework for end-to-end visual causal reasoning using pretrained foundation models. FM-CGM formalizes the causal pipeline through three core components: a concept extractor, a concept manipulator, and a counterfactual generator. By leveraging a large reasoning model for causal inference and a text-to-image diffusion model for generation, our approach enables zero-shot causal discovery, intervention, and counterfactual generation. We then develop Causal Semantic Guidance (CSG), a cross-attention-based mechanism that ensures semantic interventions propagate to descendant concepts while preserving invariant regions. We empirically show that our approach can identify plausible causal structures and is suitable for faithful counterfactual image generation.

</details>


### [196] [When One Point Is Not Enough: Addressing Ambiguous Instances in Dimensionality Reduction by Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23540)
*Diede P. M. van der Hoorn,Alessio Arleo,Fernando V. Paulovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文识别了降维投影中常被忽视的视觉伪影来源——模糊实例（在高维空间中与多个互不相似的邻域高度相似的实例），并提出一种基于图的方法通过复制模糊实例为多个投影点来减少局部邻域嵌入失真，在UMAP等局部图基降维方法上展示了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降维是一种有损过程，投影中存在视觉伪影。标准降维方法将每个数据点映射为单个点，无法忠实地投影那些与高维空间中多个互不相似邻域高度相似的模糊实例，导致仅部分邻域结构被呈现（部分邻域嵌入失真）。

Method: 提出一种基于图的方法来识别模糊实例，并将其复制为投影中的多个点，每个副本放置在其对应的邻域中。该方法基于UMAP实现，但可推广到其他局部图基降维技术。

Result: 该方法揭示了投影中先前隐藏的邻域成员关系，在多个示例中减少了部分邻域嵌入失真，并通过定量分析得到了支持。

Conclusion: 所提出方法能有效处理降维中模糊实例导致的视觉伪影，为更忠实的降维可视化提供了新途径，且具有通用性。

Abstract: Dimensionality Reduction (DR) methods are widely used to visualize high-dimensional data. One key task in DR-based analysis is discovering neighborhoods, which relies on analyzing the fine-grained local structure of a projection. However, DR is an inherently lossy process; no technique can perfectly preserve the high-dimensional relationships, and projections therefore contain visual artifacts. In this paper, we highlight a typically overlooked source of visual artifacts: ambiguous instances. These are instances that are highly similar to multiple mutually dissimilar neighborhoods in the high-dimensional space. Standard DR methods cannot faithfully project such instances, since each data instance is mapped to a single point in the visual space. As a result, such an instance is placed in only one of its neighborhoods (or in none at all), so only part of its neighborhood structure is represented. We call this distortion partial neighborhood embedding. In this paper, we introduce a graph-based approach that identifies ambiguous instances and replicates them as multiple points in the projection, placing each copy within its respective neighborhood. We use UMAP for our results, but our approach also generalizes to other local graph-based DR techniques, and we show that our approach reveals previously hidden neighborhood memberships in projections and reduces partial neighborhood embedding across multiple examples, and is further supported by quantitative analyses.

</details>


### [197] [Is Dimensionality a Barrier for Retrieval Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23556)
*Kiril Bangachev,Guy Bresler,Jonathan Kogan,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究嵌入检索模型中表示维度与最大边距的关系，证明维度 d=O(m^{-2} log n) 即可逼近无限维下的最优边距，并针对 k-稀疏查询矩阵给出维度下界 O(k log(n/k))，同时实验表明 sigmoid 损失优于 InfoNCE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代嵌入检索模型通常使用低维表示（d≈1000）却能处理数十亿甚至万亿级数据点，这一现象缺乏理论解释。本文旨在从最大边距的角度，回答低维度为何不影响大规模检索的可扩展性。

Method: 基于通信复杂度中经典的最大边距检索模型，分析给定相关矩阵 A 时，存在单位范数嵌入使得正相关对的内积 ≥ m、负相关对的内积 ≤ -m 的最大可能边距 m。通过定理证明维度与边距的关系，并给出上下界。

Result: 主要定理表明，无限维下的最优边距 m 可在维度 d=O(m^{-2} log n) 中近似达到；对于所有 k-稀疏行构成的矩阵，维度 d=Θ(k log(n/k)) 是达到最大边距 Θ(k^{-1/2}) 的充分必要条件。实验验证 sigmoid 损失比 InfoNCE 损失更有利于产生大边距嵌入。

Conclusion: 低维表示足以支持大规模检索的理论原因在于，所需维度仅与最优边距的平方成反比且对数依赖于数据规模，而非线性增长。这解释了现代嵌入检索模型的可扩展性，并提供了损失函数选择的指导。

Abstract: Why does the low dimensionality of representations, typically $d\approx 1000$, not prevent modern embedding-based retrieval models from scaling to billions, or even trillions, of data points? To answer this question, we study maximal-margin embeddings in the following retrieval model, classically studied in communication complexity [PS86] and more recently in embedding-based retrieval [WBNL26]. Let $A\in \{0,1\}^{N\times n}$ be a matrix indicating whether each of $N$ queries is relevant to each of $n$ documents. We are interested in the largest margin $m>0,$ denoted by $\mathsf{m}^{\mathsf{rd}}(d, A),$ for which there exist unit norm embeddings of the queries and documents $\{U_j\}_{j = 1}^N, \{V_i\}_{i = 1}^n$ with the following property. $\langle U_j, V_i\rangle \ge m$ whenever $A_{ji} = 1$ and $\langle U_j, V_i\rangle \le -m$ otherwise. A large margin is a key proxy for representation quality: it controls both robustness to perturbations and compositional generalization across queries. Our main theorem establishes that the best possible margin without a restriction on the dimension, $\mathsf{m}^{\mathsf{rd}}(+\infty, A),$ can be nearly achieved in dimension $d = O(\mathsf{m}^{\mathsf{rd}}(+\infty, A)^{-2}\log n)$ which improves a theorem of [BDES02]. Together with a matching lower bound in Theorem 1.5, we conclude that when $A\in \{0,1\}^{\binom{n}{k}\times n}$ is the matrix containing all possible $k$-sparse rows once, dimension $d = O(k\log (n/k))$ is necessary and sufficient for the maximal possible margin $\mathsf{m}^{\mathsf{rd}}(+\infty, A) = Θ(k^{-1/2})$ in this setting. This fully resolves the setup of [WBNL26]. We also give several constructions for large margins when $d = o(k\log (n/k)).$ Finally, we empirically test the InfoNCE and sigmoid losses for producing large margin embeddings and demonstrate a clear advantage of the sigmoid loss.

</details>


### [198] [MARS: Magnitude-Aware Rank Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23563)
*Muhammad Rajabinasab,Afsaneh M. Nejad,Arthur Zimek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出MARS方法，通过在CD图中加入相对边际系数权重，解决了标准排名忽略性能差距大小的问题，从而更真实地反映模型性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准临界差（CD）图使用离散排名，忽略了模型间性能差距的大小，导致‘幅度盲’问题，需要一种能保留差距大小的统计方法。

Method: 提出Magnitude-Aware Rank Statistics（MARS），引入相对边际系数作为离散排名的权重，该系数基于最佳与最差性能之间的距离动态缩放排名，并通过动态投影处理边界情况。

Result: MARS通过计算CD值，提供了更真实的模型性能差异统计表示，在广泛实验设置中揭示了更多关于方法实际表现的信息。

Conclusion: MARS通过考虑性能差距幅度，有效改进了标准CD图，使模型比较更准确且信息更丰富。

Abstract: Comprehensive evaluation of machine learning models is the key to make sure that they perform as robustly and consistently as desired. In order to summarize the experimental results and pick a winner, Critical Difference (CD) diagrams are used. Standard CD diagrams rely on discrete ranks, discarding the magnitude of performance gaps between models, raising an issue which we call magnitude-blindness. In order to address this issue, we propose Magnitude-Aware Rank Statistics (MARS) that incorporates a relative margin coefficient as a weight for the discrete ranks. This coefficient scales ranks based on the distance between the best and worst performers, with a dynamic projection to handle boundary cases. Followed by the calculation of a CD value, MARS results in a more realistic statistical representation of differences of model performances and more insights on how methods actually perform in vast and extensive experimental settings.

</details>


### [199] [Push Your Agent: Measuring and Enforcing Quantitative Goal Persistence in Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23574)
*Yuandao Cai,Yuzhang Zhu,Liyou Gao,Wensheng Tang,Shengchao Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PushBench量化评估了长程语言智能体在定量目标持久性（QGP）方面的表现，发现即使能完成局部工具调用，智能体也难以坚持到外部验证器确认足够数量的有效成果；状态追踪控制器消除重复提交后达69-78%成功率，积压追踪控制器在标准控制器完全失败的环境中达25-50%成功率，而黑盒前沿智能体（Claude Code、Codex CLI）在50工件任务中表现良好，但在100工件任务中成功率降至每条件3/9。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程语言智能体在复杂任务中能够完成许多合理的局部工具调用，却难以坚持到请求的计数目标真正完成。这种"量化目标持久性"（QGP）的差距被现有评估指标所掩盖——传统方法仅关注最终成功标志，无法直接测量重复工作、重复提交、虚假完成和进度漂移等问题。

Method: 构建PushBench基准测试，基于仓库工件收集和验证器支持的工作单元来量化测量QGP。设计了多种控制器进行对比：状态追踪检索控制器、积压追踪工作单元控制器、标准控制器和完成门控控制器。同时在Claude Code (Sonnet 4.6)和Codex CLI (gpt-5.4)等黑盒前沿智能体上进行评估。

Result: 状态追踪检索控制器达到69-78%成功率并消除重复提交；积压追踪工作单元控制器在标准控制器和完成门控控制器完全失败的环境中达到25-50%成功率。黑盒前沿智能体在50工件任务中解决良好，但在100工件任务中每条件仅3/9成功。

Conclusion: 定量目标对智能体的可靠性要求与局部任务能力不同：智能体必须维护经过验证的进度，并仅在请求的工作完全完成时停止。不同控制器和智能体在QGP上的表现差异表明，这是一个独立于局部任务完成能力的关键挑战维度。

Abstract: Long-horizon language agents can make many plausible local tool calls yet fail to persist until a requested count is actually complete. We study this gap as Quantitative Goal Persistence (QGP): whether an agent keeps working until an external verifier confirms enough distinct valid items. PushBench turns this into a benchmark for repository-artifact collection and verifier-backed work units, so repeated work, duplicate submissions, false completion, and progress drift are measured directly rather than hidden behind a final success flag. In matched controller comparisons, a state-tracking retrieval controller reaches 69-78% success while eliminating duplicate submissions, and a backlog-tracking work-unit controller reaches 25-50% success in settings where standard and completion-gated controllers complete no task instances. Black-box frontier-agent evaluations with Claude Code (Sonnet 4.6) and Codex CLI (gpt-5.4) solve many 50-artifact tasks but drop to 3 out of 9 successes per condition at 100 artifacts. The results show that quantitative goals stress a different reliability requirement from local task competence: agents must maintain verified progress and stop only when the requested work is complete.

</details>


### [200] [Preisach Attention: A Hysteretic Model of Sequential Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23603)
*Piotr Frydrych*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Preisach注意力层（PAL），一种基于经典Preisach磁滞算子的新型序列建模架构，用二进制继电算子替代softmax注意力机制，通过维护局部极值栈作为内部状态，实现了O(1)深度的图灵完备性，且与Transformer的计算函数类不可比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer的注意力机制存在O(n²)的计算复杂度，且需要O(log n)深度才能实现图灵完备；此外，许多序列建模任务（如长程 episodic 记忆和弱位置依赖任务）不需要绝对位置编码，但现有架构无法高效处理。PAL旨在提供一种与位置无关、计算高效且表达能力与Transformer互补的替代方案。

Method: 将经典物理学中的Preisach磁滞算子引入深度学习，用可学习激活/去激活阈值的二进制继电算子替代softmax注意力；通过维护局部极值栈作为内部状态来记录序列历史；通过模拟双栈下推自动机实现O(1)深度的图灵完备性。

Result: 单层PAL-Transformer在任意精度算术下具有图灵完备性（O(1)深度 vs 标准硬注意力Transformer的O(log n)深度）；证明PAL与Transformer可计算的函数类不可比较——PAL可在O(1)层计算历史范围统计量（Transformer需O(log n)层），而Transformer支持PAL无法实现的随机访问检索；分离性质为速率无关性（rate-independence）；极值栈构成所有速率无关泛函的输入历史的最小充分统计量；PAL推理开销为O(n log n) vs 标准注意力的O(n²)。

Conclusion: PAL是一种对长程episodic记忆和弱位置依赖任务高效的架构，在计算复杂度和表达能力上与标准Transformer形成互补关系，为序列建模提供了新的理论视角和实用选择。

Abstract: We introduce the Preisach Attention Layer (PAL), a novel sequence modelling architecture grounded in the classical Preisach hysteresis operator from mathematical physics. PAL replaces the softmax attention mechanism with a binary relay operator parameterised by learned activation and deactivation thresholds, maintaining a stack of local extrema as its internal state. A single-layer PAL-Transformer with O(1) depth is Turing-complete under arbitrary precision arithmetic, achievable through simulation of a two-stack pushdown automaton -- in contrast to the O(log n) depth required by standard hard-attention transformers. Second, we prove that the function classes computable by PAL and by the transformer are incomparable: PAL computes historical range statistics in O(1) layers that require O(log n) layers for transformers, while transformers support random-access retrieval that PAL cannot perform without auxiliary state. The separating property is rate-independence -- PAL responds only to the sequence of local extrema, not to absolute token positions or temporal spacing. Third, we show that the extremum stack constitutes a minimal sufficient statistic of the input history for all rate-independent functionals, providing a formal analogue of the wiping property in classical hysteresis theory. PAL is thus an efficient architecture for tasks with long episodic memory and weak positional dependence, with O(n log n) total inference cost versus O(n^2) for standard attention.

</details>


### [201] [Valid and Expressive Copulas for Irregular Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23632)
*Christian Klötergens,Tom Hanika,Lars Schmidt-Thieme,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CopFITi是一种用于非规则多元时间序列概率预测的Copula模型，通过结合归一化流（边际）和高斯混合Copula（联合依赖）实现灵活性、一致性与SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非规则多元时间序列（IMTS）的联合概率建模面临挑战：既需要灵活拟合每个变量的边际分布，又需要准确刻画变量间的依赖结构。现有方法（如直接拟合联合分布）可能会在边际建模上妥协。

Method: 提出CopFITi模型，采用解耦策略：用归一化流（Normalizing Flows）灵活建模每个变量的单变量边际分布，用高斯混合Copula（Gaussian Mixture Copula）构建一致且灵活的联合依赖结构，并确保模型具有边际化一致性（marginalization-consistent）。

Result: 实验表明，Copula方法（解耦边际与联合）相比直接拟合联合分布的架构，能得到更好的边际模型；CopFITi成为首个满足边际化一致性的IMTS Copula模型，在联合IMTS密度建模上达到新SOTA。

Conclusion: CopFITi通过解耦边际和联合分布，有效提升了非规则多元时间序列的联合概率预测质量，验证了Copula框架在该任务上的理论优势和实践有效性。

Abstract: We introduce CopFITi, a copula model for probabilistic forecasting of irregular multivariate time series (IMTS). Our model combines the expressivity of normalizing flows for univariate marginals with the consistency and flexibility of a Gaussian Mixture Copula for the joint dependency structure. Our experiments show that copula-based approaches, which decouple the marginals from the joint, yield better marginal models than architectures that directly fit the full joint. With CopFITi, we propose the first IMTS copula that is marginalization-consistent by construction and establish a new state of the art in joint IMTS density modeling.

</details>


### [202] [Optimal Dimension-Free Sampling for Regularized Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23726)
*Meysam Alishahi,Alexander Munteanu,Simon Omlor,Jeff M. Phillips*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对一类广泛的Lipschitz连续分类损失函数（包括逻辑损失、sigmoid损失、hinge损失和ReLU损失等），证明了在多种正则化项下达到(1±ε)相对误差的最优采样界。对于ℓ2/k正则化得到k²/ε²的上下界，对于ℓ1/k正则化得到k/ε²的上下界。当损失函数满足有界导数性质时，ℓ2²/k正则化可实现线性k的采样复杂度；否则为k²/ε²。若g(0)=0，则无维度自由界。所有上界均有匹配的下界。该方法基于简单均匀或（平方）范数采样，改进了近期灵敏度采样的k³/ε²界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分类损失函数的采样复杂度分析通常依赖于VC维度和灵敏度框架，导致过计数问题（如Alishahi和Phillips在ICML'24中给出的k³/ε²界）。本文旨在通过更精细的论证，为广泛的Lipschitz连续损失函数建立更紧的、最优的采样界，特别是消除维度依赖并突破灵敏度采样的立方上界。

Method: 采用简单均匀采样或（平方）范数采样，结合高阶矩界和经验过程分析，避免VC维度和灵敏度框架中的过计数问题。对于ℓ2/k正则化使用均匀采样，ℓ1/k正则化使用范数采样，ℓ2²/k正则化则根据损失函数导数性质分别处理。

Result: 1) ℓ2/k正则化：k²/ε²上下界；2) ℓ1/k正则化：k/ε²上下界；3) ℓ2²/k正则化：若满足|g'(x)|≤g(x)、g(0)>0且g单调或凸，则采样复杂度为O(k)；否则为k²/ε²；4) 若g(0)=0，则无维度自由界；5) 所有上界均有匹配的下界（至多差多项式对数因子）；6) 将边界从k³/ε²改进至k²/ε²或更好。

Conclusion: 本文为Lipschitz连续分类损失函数建立了最优（匹配上下界）的采样复杂度理论，揭示了不同正则化项下采样复杂度的本质差异，特别是g(0)>0这一条件对维度自由界的必要性。算法上，简单的均匀/范数采样即可达到最优，无需复杂采样策略。

Abstract: We prove optimal sampling bounds achieving $(1\pm\varepsilon)$-relative error for a broad class of Lipschitz continuous classification loss functions under various regularization terms. This includes important functions such as logistic and sigmoid loss, hinge loss, and ReLU loss, as prominent and popular representative examples. In particular, we prove $k^2/\varepsilon^2$ upper and lower bounds for $\|\cdot\|_2/k$ regularization, and $k/\varepsilon^2$ upper and lower bounds for $\|\cdot\|_1/k$ regularization. For $\|\cdot\|_2^2/k$ regularization, the sampling complexity depends mainly on a bounded derivative property: if $|g'(x)|\leq g(x)$, and $g(0)>0$, and $g$ is monotonic or convex, then it admits linear in $k$ sampling complexity; otherwise the general bound is $k^2/\varepsilon^2$. However, if $g(0)=0$, our results indicate that no dimension-free bounds are possible, and even sublinear bounds are ruled out. All upper bounds are complemented by matching lower bounds up to polylogarithmic terms. Moreover, our work relies conceptually and algorithmically on simple uniform or (squared) norm sampling and hereby improves over recent cubic $k^3/\varepsilon^2$ sensitivity sampling bounds of (Alishahi and Phillips, ICML'24). This is achieved by refined arguments involving higher moment bounds and empirical process analyses to avoid overcounting that appears in the de-facto standard VC-dimension and sensitivity framework.

</details>


### [203] [It's the humans, not the data: Geopolitical bias in LLMs originates in post-training, amplified by the language of the prompt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23825)
*Stuart Bladon,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 地缘政治偏见并非源于预训练阶段，而是源于后训练（对齐）阶段。七个AI实验室中有六个的聊天模型在训练后表现出对模型开发者所在国家/地区的偏好偏向，且这种偏向的幅度受提示语言影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验一种普遍假设：语言模型中的地缘政治偏见来自于预训练阶段使用的训练数据。

Method: 对七个实验室的七对开源大模型（基础模型仅预训练，聊天模型经过预训练+后训练）进行配对场景强制选择探测，涉及28个国家配对，使用英语、法语和中文三种语言。

Result: 地缘政治偏见主要源于后训练阶段而非预训练阶段。七个AI实验室中有六个在聊天模型（后训练后）表现出向模型开发者所在国家/地区的方向偏移。这种偏移在阿里的Qwen 2.5中最为显著：基础模型对中国偏好呈中性，而后训练的聊天模型偏好大幅增强。偏移幅度受提示语言影响：法国Mistral模型仅在法语提示下才表现出亲法偏见。

Conclusion: 语言模型的地缘政治偏好并非简单继承自大规模互联网数据，而是在后训练阶段被主动塑造，这凸显了对影响模型如何表征国家、文化和政治观点的对齐过程进行更透明的审查和监督的必要性。

Abstract: It has generally been assumed that geopolitical bias in language models originates from the training data used during the pre-training phase. We tested seven open-weight LLM pairs consisting of the base model (pre-training only) and the chat model (pre-training and post-training) from seven labs on a paired-scenario forced-choice probe over 28 country pairs in English, French, and Chinese, and found that geopolitical bias originates in post-training rather than in pre-training. Across seven AI labs, six showed shifts in the direction associated with the country or region of the model developer after post-training. This shift is strongest in Alibaba's Qwen 2.5: while the base is neutral on China-favourability (-0.15 log-odds, p=0.15), the post-trained chat variant is at +2.91 (p<10^-4), an 18x shift in odds. We also observe shifts in biases toward other countries across all models. Additionally, the magnitude of this shift depends on the language used to prompt the model: the French-made Mistral becomes pro-France only under French prompting (FR-EN shift +1.91, p<10^-4). These findings suggest that geopolitical preferences in language models are not simply inherited from large-scale internet data but are actively shaped during post-training, highlighting the need for greater transparency, auditing, and oversight of alignment processes that influence how models represent nations, cultures, and political perspectives.

</details>


### [204] [Approaching I/O-optimality for Approximate Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23751)
*Pál András Papp,Aleksandros Sobczyk,Anastasios Zouzias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了大语言模型中注意力机制的I/O复杂度，提出了一种I/O成本几乎线性依赖于n的新方法，并给出了各参数范围内的匹配下界，证明算法接近I/O最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FlashAttention等方法的I/O复杂度与n呈二次关系，而平凡下界仅需Ω(nd)次I/O，存在巨大的理论-实践差距，因此需要缩小这一差距，设计更高效的注意力I/O算法。

Method: 受Alman和Song近期近似注意力框架启发，通过开发I/O高效算法，在大多数参数范围内使I/O成本仅与n呈近线性关系。

Result: 新算法的I/O成本在大部分参数范围内从二次降至近线性，并证明了各参数范围内的匹配下界。

Conclusion: 所提出的注意力计算方法在理论上接近I/O最优，显著改进了FlashAttention等现有方法的I/O复杂度。论文的结论是算法接近I/O最优，首次将注意力I/O复杂度从二次降至近线性。

Abstract: We revisit the I/O complexity of attention in large language models. Given query-key-value matrices $Q,K,V\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times d}$, and a machine with fast memory size $M$, the goal is to compute the "attention matrix" $A=\text{softmax}(Q K ^{\top}/\sqrt{d}) V$ with the minimal number of data transfers between fast and slow memory. Existing methods in the literature, most notably FlashAttention and its variants, incur an I/O cost that depends quadratically on $n$, while a trivial lower bound only requires $Ω(nd)$ I/O's to read the inputs and write the output. In this work, we present a technique for computing attention where the I/O cost only depends almost-linearly on $n$ in most parameter regimes. This is achieved by developing I/O-efficient algorithms inspired by the recent approximate attention framework of Alman and Song. We also prove corresponding lower bounds in each parameter regime to show that our algorithms are indeed close to I/O-optimal.

</details>


### [205] [SeedER: Seed-and-Expand Retrieval from Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23753)
*Hamed Shirzad,Frederik Wenkel,Dominique Beaini,Danica J. Sutherland,Emmanuel Noutahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SeedER（种子-扩展检索）框架，通过轻量级种子选取和图感知策略的强化学习迭代扩展，在可控成本下高效检索知识图谱中的多跳组合查询，相比密集检索和图增强基线显著提升召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱的不规则结构导致检索困难：ego-graph扩展迅速膨胀，密集嵌入方法难以处理多跳组合查询，而现有的基于智能体的图探索方法在大规模检索中成本过高。

Method: SeedER先使用轻量级密集检索和实体检索播种一个紧凑的核心节点集，再通过基于强化学习的图感知策略选择性扩展该集合，将全局推理分解为可重用的局部决策，从而在严格控制扩展成本的同时高效发现查询相关节点。

Result: 从组合泛化和图约束子模优化角度证明了SeedER的理论优势，实验表明SeedER在紧凑候选集上相比强密集和图增强基线显著提升了召回率。

Conclusion: SeedER是一种有效的第一阶段检索器，适用于知识密集型推理系统，通过显式利用知识图谱结构实现了低成本、高召回的组合查询检索。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs (KGs) offer a rich representation for relational knowledge, but their irregular structure makes retrieval challenging: ego-graph expansion grows rapidly, and dense embedding methods struggle with multi-hop compositional queries. Existing agent-based graph exploration approaches, while expressive, are often too expensive for large-scale retrieval. We introduce SeedER (Seed-and-Expand Retrieval), a retrieval framework that explicitly leverages KG structure through iterative, low-cost expansion. SeedER first seeds a compact set of core nodes using lightweight dense and entity-based retrieval, then selectively expands this set via a learned graph-aware policy trained with reinforcement learning. This design decomposes global reasoning into reusable local decisions, enabling efficient discovery of query-relevant nodes while tightly controlling expansion cost. We show theoretical limitations of dense retrieval on compositional graph queries, and establish advantages of SeedER from both compositional generalization and graph-constrained submodular optimization perspectives. Empirically, SeedER substantially improves recall with compact candidate sets over strong dense and graph-augmented baselines, making it an effective first-stage retriever for knowledge-intensive reasoning systems.

</details>


### [206] [LLMs as Noisy Channels: A Shannon Perspective on Model Capacity and Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23901)
*Xu Ouyang,Deyi Liu,Yuhang Cai,Jing Liu,Yuan Yang,Chen Zheng,Thomas Hartvigsen,Yiyuan Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出香农缩放定律，将LLM训练建模为噪声信道中的信息传输，统一解释了现有缩放定律无法涵盖的非单调现象（如灾难性过训练和量化退化）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM缩放定律无法解释非单调现象（如灾难性过训练和量化退化），且缺乏统一的数学框架来捕捉学习信号与内在噪声之间的相互作用。

Method: 基于香农-哈特利定理，将模型参数映射为信道带宽、训练token映射为信号功率，构建香农缩放定律框架，通过信噪比(SNR)的统一视角推导出LLM的香农容量。

Result: 在Pythia和OLMo2上的高斯噪声、量化和SFT实验表明，新定律在R²指标上优于经典缩放定律和近期扰动感知定律；在6.9B模型上拟合后在12B模型上外推至307B token时仍达到R²=0.847，而单调基线完全失效。

Conclusion: 香农缩放定律为LLM性能退化（包括灾难性过训练和量化退化）提供了统一的理论解释和外推能力，揭示了缩放过程中维持足够信噪比的重要性。

Abstract: Existing scaling laws for Large Language Models (LLMs), predominantly monotonic power laws, fail to explain emerging non-monotonic phenomena such as catastrophic overtraining and quantization-induced degradation, where performance deteriorates despite increased compute.
  We propose the Shannon Scaling Law, a unified theoretical framework that models LLM training as information transmission over a noisy channel, grounded in the Shannon-Hartley theorem. By mapping model parameters to channel bandwidth and training tokens to signal power, our formulation explicitly captures the interaction between learning signal and intrinsic noise. This perspective reveals a fundamental Shannon capacity for LLMs: scaling model size or data without preserving a sufficient signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) inevitably amplifies noise, inducing a transition from monotonic improvement to U-shaped performance degradation.
  We validate our theory through experiments on Pythia and OLMo2 under perturbations, including Gaussian noise, quantization and supervised fine-tuning on math, QA and code tasks. The Shannon Scaling Law consistently outperforms classical scaling laws and recent perturbation-aware laws, achieving strong $R^2$ scores and accurately capturing loss basins missed by prior approaches. It also extrapolates: fitted on $\leq$6.9B Pythia models with $\leq$180B tokens, it predicts the unseen 12B model up to 307B tokens at pooled $R^2{=}0.847$, while monotonic baselines collapse.

</details>


### [207] [Training-Free Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23872)
*Lizhang Chen,Jonathan Li,Chen Liang,Ni Lao,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的循环Transformer方法，通过在测试时对冻结检查点的中间块进行循环推理，采用阻尼子步骤替代单一大更新，在多个模型上提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环Transformer方法需要端到端训练或微调，缺乏对预训练模型的零成本推理增强方案。且简单块复用通常导致性能下降，需要设计有效的循环策略。

Method: 将预归一化Transformer块视为ODE的前向欧拉步，将循环视为对该近似的细化——用多个较小的阻尼子步骤替代单一大更新，无需额外训练或架构修改。

Result: 在7个密集、稀疏MoE及MLA+MoE模型家族上取得提升：Qwen3-4B-Instruct在MMLU-Pro上+2.64 pp，Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct在CommonsenseQA上+1.14 pp，Moonlight-16B-A3B-Instruct在OpenBookQA上+1.20 pp。

Conclusion: 训练无关的循环策略能够显著提升冻结预训练模型的推理性能，且方法简单通用，适用于多种模型架构。

Abstract: We introduce training-free looped transformers, in which a lightweight inference-time wrapper loops a contiguous mid-stack block of layers of a frozen checkpoint without additional fine-tuning, continued training, or architectural changes. Unlike prior looped transformer methods that train with the looped structure end-to-end, we retrofit recurrence onto pretrained models at test time. We show that naive block reapplication usually degrades performance, highlighting the importance of the loop application strategy. Motivated by viewing a pre-norm transformer block as a forward Euler step on an ODE, we instead treat looping as a refinement of the same approximation, replacing one large update with smaller damped sub-steps. Across seven dense, sparse MoE, and MLA+MoE model families, our method improves Qwen3-4B-Instruct by +2.64 pp on MMLU-Pro, Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct by +1.14 pp on CommonsenseQA, and Moonlight-16B-A3B-Instruct by +1.20 pp on OpenBookQA.

</details>


### [208] [Complete-muE: Optimal Hyperparameter Transfer and Scaling for MoE Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23893)
*Hongwu Peng,Ohiremen Dibua,Yuanjun Xiong,Yifan Gong,Jianming Zhang,Yan Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Complete-muE是一个框架，通过双桥系统（Bridge I：密集FFN到密集MoE的主动宽度μP+归一化路由缩放；Bridge II：密集MoE到稀疏MoE的激活专家缩放+一阶SDE LR/WD校正+有界残差σ₀偏移）实现超参数在密集FFN与任意MoE架构间的迁移。规则覆盖专家数量、容量、粒度、共享/分组平衡混合及通用Transformer的宽度/深度/批量/时长变化。实验证实迁移后超参数最优值仅存在微小漂移，实现"一次调优密集模型，迁移至所有MoE配置"，使MoE模型加速收敛且无需昂贵搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具（如μP需固定架构，SDE需固定每步token数）无法直接解决MoE设置中的超参数迁移问题，因为Dense到MoE迁移或MoE总专家数扩展同时改变了架构和每个专家的token数。

Method: 提出Complete-muE双桥系统：Bridge I通过主动宽度μP和归一化路由缩放实现密集FFN到密集MoE的映射；Bridge II通过激活专家缩放实现密集MoE到稀疏MoE的映射，其中一阶SDE LR/WD校正相互抵消，仅保留有界残差σ₀偏移。

Result: 大量语言模型和扩散模型预训练实验证实，Complete-muE在不同模型架构和参数量下能产生相对稳定的超参数最优值，仅存在与Bridge II非严格SDE行为一致的微小漂移。

Conclusion: 实践中漂移足够小，以至于在单个密集参考模型上调优的超参数可以近最优地迁移到所有MoE配置，实现了"一次调优密集模型，迁移至所有MoE配置"的实用方案，使MoE模型在扩展容量时实现加速收敛，无需昂贵的超参数搜索。

Abstract: We propose Complete-muE, a framework which targets hyperparameter transfer across dense FFN and any Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) setups in transformer blocks. Existing tools such as $μ$P (requires fixed architectue) or SDE (requires fixed per-step token count) cannot directly solve the hyperparameter transfer problem in MoE setups because Dense to MoE transfer or MoE total experts scaling changes both architecture and tokens per expert. Complete-muE solves this challenge with a two-bridge system: Bridge~I maps between dense FFN and Dense MoE by active-width $μ$P with a normalized router scale. Bridge~II maps between Dense MoE and sparse MoE by activated-expert scaling, where the first-order SDE LR/WD correction cancels while a bounded residual $σ_0$ shift remains. The resulting transfer rule, which we term as Complete muE, covers changes in activated experts, total capacity, granularity, and shared/group-balanced hybrids for MoE models as well as network width/depth, batch size, and duration changes for general Transformer models. Extensive language model and diffusion model pretraining experiments confirm that complete-muE yields relatively stable hyperparameter optima across model architectures and parameter counts -- with only minor drift consistent with the non-strict SDE behavior of Bridge~II. In practice this drift is small enough that hyperparameters tuned on a single dense reference transfer near-optimally to all MoE configurations -- \emph{tune dense once, transfer to all} is the practical recipe at the core of Complete-muE. This enables MoE models to achieve accelerated convergence speedup over dense models when scaling model capacity without costly hyperparameter search.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [209] [RMA: an Agentic System for Research-Level Mathematical Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22875)
*Zelin Zhao,Bo Yuan,Jaemoo Choi,Yongxin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一个名为Research Math Agents (RMA)的智能体框架，专门用于自动化解决研究级数学问题，通过多智能体协作、结构化记忆和迭代精炼，在First Proof benchmark上解决了十个问题中的八个，显著优于GPT-5.2R和Aletheia等强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多聚焦于竞赛数学或形式化定理证明，缺乏针对需要长程推理、文献支撑和迭代证明精炼的研究级数学问题的自动化方法。

Method: 将研究级证明求解分解为问题分析、文献搜索理解、公平比较、知识库构建和证明验证等专门模块，由初始化器、提议器和验证器三个智能体通过共享结构化记忆进行协调，在统一框架下执行多角色、多轮次的工作流，协同生成、精炼和验证候选证明。

Result: 在包含十个跨领域研究级问题的First Proof benchmark上，RMA解决了八个问题，超越了GPT-5.2R和Aletheia等强基线，生成的证明在逻辑性和可读性上更优。

Conclusion: 性能提升源于结构化推理模块、迭代精炼和基于验证器的反馈三者之间的交互作用，而非单一组件贡献；框架和实现将在论文接收后公开。

Abstract: We present $\textbf{Research Math Agents (RMA)}$, an agentic framework for automated reasoning on research-level mathematical problems. Unlike prior studies centered on competition mathematics or formal theorem proving, RMA targets research-level mathematical problems that require long-horizon reasoning, literature grounding, and iterative proof refinement. RMA decomposes research-level proof solving into specialized modules for problem analysis, literature search and understanding, fair comparison, knowledge-bank construction, and proof verification, all coordinated by initializer, proposer, and verifier agents through a shared structured memory. Within this unified framework, these agents operate in a multi-role, multi-round workflow, collaboratively generating, refining, and verifying candidate proofs through iterative feedback. We evaluate RMA on the First Proof benchmark, which consists of ten research-level problems contributed by expert mathematicians across diverse domains. Through comprehensive expert evaluation, RMA outperforms strong baselines on the First Proof benchmark, including GPT-5.2R and Aletheia, solving eight out of ten research problems and producing more logically sound and readable proofs. Our comprehensive ablation studies further show that performance gains arise from the interaction of structured reasoning modules, iterative refinement, and verifier-based feedback, rather than any single component. Our solutions and implementations will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [210] [SciAtlas: A Large-Scale Knowledge Graph for Automated Scientific Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22878)
*Shuofei Qiao,Yunxiang Wei,Jiazheng Fan,Bin Wu,Busheng Zhang,Mengru Wang,Yuqi Zhu,Ningyu Zhang,Keyan Ding,Qiang Zhang,Huajun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SciAtlas是一个大规模跨学科异构图谱，整合4300万论文、1.57亿实体和30亿三元组，通过神经符号检索算法实现从语义匹配到确定性关联的跃迁，为AI智能体提供全景科研认知地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前学术检索工具依赖浅层关键词匹配或向量语义检索，缺乏拓扑推理能力，且深度研究框架存在逻辑幻觉与高推理成本问题，亟需结构化的知识组织方式。

Method: 构建跨26学科、4300万论文的异构图谱；提出三路径协同召回与图重排序的神经符号检索算法，结合拓扑认知基座实现确定性关联发现。

Result: 在文献综述、自动趋势综合、观点定位与学术轨迹探索等任务中，SciAtlas作为认知地图有效赋能全流程自动化研究，并显著降低推理成本。

Conclusion: SciAtlas通过结构化拓扑认知基座与神经符号检索，弥合了语义匹配与逻辑推理之间的鸿沟，为AI驱动的科学发现提供了高效、可解释的知识基础设施。

Abstract: The exponential growth of global academic output has confronted researchers and AI agents with an unprecedented ``information explosion,'' where fragmented and unstructured knowledge organization impedes deep interdisciplinary integration. Current academic retrieval tools predominantly rely on superficial keyword matching or vector-space semantic retrieval, which lack the topological reasoning capabilities required to navigate complex logical connections. Agentic deep-research-based frameworks are often prone to logical hallucinations and consuming high inference costs. To bridge this gap, in this report, we introduce SciAtlas, a large-scale, multi-disciplinary, heterogeneous academic resource knowledge graph designed as a panoramic scientific evolution network. By integrating over 43M papers from 26 disciplines, and a total of 157M entities and 3B triplets, SciAtlas provides a structured topological cognitive substrate that dismantles disciplinary barriers and furnishes AI agents with a global perspective. Furthermore, we develop a neuro-symbolic retrieval algorithm featuring tri-path collaborative recall and graph reranking, achieving a seamless transition from simple semantic matching to deterministic association discovery. We also present key application directions of SciAtlas, including literature review, automated research trend synthesis, idea positioning, and academic trajectory exploration, to demonstrate that SciAtlas can serve as an effective ``cognitive map'' to empower the full loop of automated scientific research while significantly reducing reasoning costs. We have released the interfaces for KG retrieval and various downstream tasks in our GitHub repo.

</details>


### [211] [ImProver 2: Iteratively Self-Improving LMs for Neurosymbolic Proof Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22885)
*Riyaz Ahuja,Tate Rowney,Jeremy Avigad,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了ImProver 2框架，通过神经符号方法结合专家迭代流水线和形式化结构暴露，在Lean 4中实现了自动证明优化，训练的小模型在多项指标上超越大规模模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化数学库快速扩张，需要重构已验证的证明以提升可维护性和训练数据质量，但可扩展的证明优化面临异构启发式目标、数据稀缺以及高训练推理成本等挑战。

Method: 采用数据高效专家迭代流水线（ImProver 2），结合暴露形式化结构及轻量级非正式抽象的脚手架，并引入一组衡量证明结构属性的指标。

Result: 训练出的7B参数模型在同类模型中超越大数个数量级的模型，与中端前沿模型竞争；神经符号脚手架显著提升小型和前沿模型性能；小模型能有效重构研究级证明。

Conclusion: 证明了通过适当的脚手架和训练，小模型可以在复杂多变的指标上有效重构研究级证明，匹配更大系统，将证明优化确立为可扩展、可学习的任务。

Abstract: Formal mathematics libraries are rapidly expanding, creating a growing need to refactor verified proofs for maintainability and to improve training data quality for neural provers. However, scalable proof optimization is hindered by heterogeneous and heuristically specified objectives, scarce data, and high training and inference costs. To overcome these challenges, we introduce ImProver 2, a neurosymbolic framework for automated proof optimization in Lean 4. ImProver 2 combines a data-efficient expert-iteration pipeline with a scaffold that exposes formal structure alongside lightweight informal abstractions. We further introduce a suite of metrics capturing structural proof properties. Using ImProver 2, we train a 7B-parameter model that outperforms orders-of-magnitude larger models within the same model family, and is competitive with mid-tier frontier models across metrics. We additionally demonstrate that our neurosymbolic scaffold significantly improves performance across both small and frontier models. We show that with proper scaffolding and training, small models can effectively restructure research-level proofs over complex and varied metrics, matching substantially larger systems and establishing proof optimization as a scalable, learnable task.

</details>


### [212] [Mediative Fuzzy Logic: From Type-1 Foundations to Type-2, Type-3 and Quantum Extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22900)
*Oscar Montiel Ross*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文为介导模糊逻辑（Mediative Fuzzy Logic）构建了统一的逻辑与语义基础，涵盖类型1、区间类型2、粒状类型3及量子扩展，建立了以犹豫和矛盾度控制的凸聚合算子、连续双格结构上的独立真-假值对模型，以及扩展标准t-模模糊逻辑的命题系统，证明了可靠性、次协调性和保守性，并通过自主制动传感器融合案例展示了其在异质、矛盾证据下支持透明、保守、安全优先决策的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 介导模糊逻辑在实际模糊控制与决策中用于调和犹豫或冲突评估，但其逻辑与语义基础（尤其在操作型类型1设置之外）发展不足。

Method: 将介导算子刻画为受犹豫和矛盾控制的凸聚合；将介导真值建模为连续双格结构上的独立真-假值对；引入扩展标准t-模模糊逻辑的命题系统，添加介导连接词；建立可靠性与次协调性证明；提出区间类型2、粒状类型3和量子扩展的语义。

Result: 建立了介导逻辑在类型1下的可靠性、次协调性和对无介导公式的保守性；高阶形式在适当假设下可归约到类型1情形，保持了跨层的一致性。

Conclusion: 该统一框架支持在不完整、异质、轻微矛盾的证据下做出透明、保守且安全优先的决策，为未来智能决策系统提供了可靠基础。

Abstract: Mediative Fuzzy Logic was conceived as a practical scheme for reconciling hesitant or conflicting assessments in fuzzy control and decision-making. However, its logical and semantic foundations remain underdeveloped, especially beyond operational type-1 settings. This article develops a unified account of the type-1 core together with interval type-2, granular type-3, and quantum extensions. We characterize the mediative operator as a convex aggregation controlled by hesitation and contradiction, model mediative truth values as independent truth-falsity pairs in a continuous bilattice-like structure, and introduce a propositional system extending a standard t-norm-based fuzzy logic with a mediative connective. We establish soundness, paraconsistency, and conservativity over the underlying fuzzy base for formulas without mediation, and formulate coherent semantic extensions to interval type-2 truth values, granule-indexed local evaluations, and effects and density operators on Hilbert spaces. An autonomous-braking sensor-fusion example illustrates how the framework supports transparent, conservative, and safety-first decisions under incomplete, heterogeneous, and mildly contradictory evidence. Under suitable assumptions, the higher-level formulations reduce to the type-1 case, clarifying coherence across levels and reliably supporting future work in intelligent decision systems.

</details>


### [213] [PathCal: State-Aware Reflection-Marker Calibration for Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23074)
*Lingyu Jiang,Zirui Li,Shuo Xing,Peiran Li,Tsubasa Takahashi,Dengzhe Hou,Zhengzhong Tu,Kazunori Yamada,Fangzhou Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PathCal是一种无训练的解码控制器，通过区分推理标记类型并在局部不确定状态进行干预，在保持或提升推理准确率的同时减少生成长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将推理过程中的反思标记（如'wait'、'but'、'alternatively'）视为单一粗粒度类别，忽略了它们不同的功能角色和影响时机，导致无法精细控制推理路径。

Method: 提出PathCal方法：在每个解码步骤利用反思标记的分布估计当前推理轨迹与竞争分支之间的局部竞争程度，当竞争分支证据过强时软性地重新平衡标记logits。

Result: 在六个推理基准上，PathCal实现了更好的效率-性能权衡：在保持或提升准确率的同时减少生成长度，且无需外部验证器或额外采样。

Conclusion: 区分反思标记类型并在局部不确定状态进行干预，能够在不牺牲准确率的前提下有效压缩推理链长度，提升推理效率。

Abstract: The emergence of Large Reasoning Language Models (LRMs) has paved the way for tackling complex reasoning tasks through test-time scaling by generating long-form Chain-of-Thought (CoT) trajectories during inference. Meanwhile, these trajectories often contain explicit reflection markers such as ``wait'', ``but'', and ``alternatively'', signaling hesitation, revision, and the consideration of alternative explorations, respectively. Recent studies on test-time control leverage such markers as lightweight handles for steering reasoning, typically treating them as a single coarse-grained category rather than distinguishing their distinct functional roles. In this paper, we conduct type-wise suppression and fixed-prefix intervention, revealing that reflection markers differ not only in their functional roles but also in when they exert the greatest influence. Specifically, different marker classes affect accuracy and generation length in distinct ways, and marker choices are most consequential before the model settles into a stable reasoning trajectory. Motivated by these findings, we introduce PathCal, a novel training-free decoding controller that calibrates reasoning paths by distinguishing marker types and intervening only at locally uncertain states. At each decoding step, PathCal utilizes the distribution over reflection-markers to estimate local competition between maintaining the current reasoning trajectory and initiating a competing branch, and softly rebalances marker logits when competing-branch evidence becomes excessive. Experiments across six reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that PathCal achieves a better efficiency--performance trade-off, improving or preserving accuracy while reducing generation length, without relying on external verifiers or additional sampling.

</details>


### [214] [Inductive Deductive Synthesis: Enabling AI to Generate Formally Verified Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23109)
*Shubham Agarwal,Alexander Krentsel,Shu Liu,Mert Cemri,Audrey Cheng,Rui Meng,Tomas Pfister,Chun-Liang Li,Sylvia Ratnasamy,Aditya Parameswaran,Matei Zaharia,Ion Stoica,Mohsen Lesani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了归纳演绎综合（IDS）方法，首次实现了LLM智能体在分布式系统形式化验证上的突破，以约6.8小时和$106的平均成本完成7/7的规格验证，比专家手动验证快约200倍，比SOTA智能体便宜17%，且能生成性能更优的实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体在代码生成、测试和优化方面表现出色，但在需要形式化全覆盖保证（如分布式系统的一致性属性）的任务上表现不佳。机械化的形式化验证虽能保证正确性，但通常需要数月到数年的专家工作。即使是SOTA编码智能体（如Codex+GPT-5.4和Claude Code+Opus 4.6）在分布式键值存储规格验证上也仅成功2/7。因此，需要一种有效的方法来弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出了归纳演绎综合（IDS）方法，该方法联合且增量地综合实现和证明，从失败的尝试中学习以系统性地尝试有前景的策略。IDS被构建为智能体LLM系统。

Result: IDS在平均约6.8小时和$106每规格的成本下实现了7/7的验证成功率，比专家手动验证快约200倍，比SOTA智能体便宜17%。此外，IDS将性能反馈纳入同一循环，生成的实现比已发布的验证系统快达3倍。

Conclusion: IDS首次实现了LLM智能体在分布式系统形式化验证任务上的高效突破，不仅大幅降低了时间和成本，还通过性能反馈循环生成了更优的实现，证明了在需要形式化保证的任务上AI可以超越纯测试驱动的方法。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly excel at generating, testing, and refining code. However, they fall short on tasks requiring formal guarantees of full coverage that testing alone cannot provide. Distributed systems are a prime example: properties such as consistency between reads and writes must hold under every possible interleaving of events. Mechanized formal verification can guarantee such correctness, but typically demands months to years of expert effort. As evidence, even SOTA coding agents (Codex with GPT-5.4 and Claude Code with Opus 4.6) succeed on only 2/7 distributed key-value-store specifications. In this paper, we present the first effective approach to addressing this gap, Inductive Deductive Synthesis (IDS), which jointly and incrementally synthesizes implementation and proof, and learns from failed attempts to systematically try promising strategies. Built as an agentic LLM system, IDS achieves 7/7 in about 6.8 hours and $106 per spec on average, roughly 200x faster than expert effort and 17% cheaper than SOTA agents. IDS further incorporates performance feedback into the same loop, yielding implementations up to 3x faster than published verified systems.

</details>


### [215] [AutoResearch AI: Towards AI-Powered Research Automation for Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23204)
*Guiyao Tie,Jiawen Shi,Dingjie Song,Yixiao Huang,Ziji Sheng,Xueyang Zhou,Daizong Liu,Pan Zhou,Yongchao Chen,Ran Xu,Lifang He,Qingsong Wen,Manling Li,Cong Lu,Shuai Li,Pengtao Xie,Yixuan Yuan,Rui Meng,Lei Xing,Lichao Sun,Caiming Xiong,Philip S. Yu,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了AI驱动的科学工作流自动化（AutoResearch）的发展谱系，涵盖从人类主导的提示式辅助（Vibe Research）到自主AI主导的科研闭环，分析了五个关键工作流阶段、评估维度及领域条件约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI科研系统虽然能力增强但呈碎片化，缺乏统一框架来理解不同自主程度、领域范围和验证机制下的工作流自动化，尤其在证据保留、可重复性、溯源追踪和问责闭环等方面存在不足，亟需系统性梳理与评估标准。

Method: 通过文献综述方式，定义AutoResearch概念谱系，划分Vibe Research（人类主导）和AI主导系统两类范式；从控制、证据、执行、验证与问责五个维度分析工作流重塑；组织五个工作流条件（文献与研究基础、假设形成与规划、实验与工具使用、反馈验证与评审、报告与知识传播）；并综合评测基准、领域部署和开源基础设施。最后提出五维评估框架（新颖性、有效性、影响力、可靠性、可溯源性）。

Result: 指出AutoResearch的自主性具有领域条件性——在结构化、可执行且可快速验证的环境中可信度更高，而在具身性、延迟反馈、异质性、伦理或机构问责的上下文中仍受限。

Conclusion: AI科研自动化的发展处于从任务级辅助向工作流级自动化的转型期，未来研究需聚焦弱方向拒绝、跨领域鲁棒性和可责信的科研闭环等挑战，并依据领域特性设计适度的自主程度。

Abstract: Scientific research is being reshaped by AI systems that move beyond isolated assistance toward longer-horizon workflows spanning literature grounding, hypothesis generation, experimentation, validation, reporting, and revision. This shift marks a transition from task-level AI for science to workflow-level research automation. Yet current systems remain fragmented, differing in autonomy, domain scope, execution environment, validation mechanism, and human oversight, while still struggling with evidence preservation, reproducibility, weak-direction rejection, provenance tracking, cross-domain robustness, and accountable scientific closure. This survey examines these developments through AutoResearch, defined as the developmental spectrum of AI-powered scientific workflow automation. Within it, Vibe Research denotes the human-steered region of prompt-based assistance and human-verified execution, whereas emerging AI-led systems coordinate larger portions of the discovery loop without achieving robust autonomy. We analyze how research systems redistribute control, evidence, execution, validation, and accountability across workflows and organize the field around five workflow conditions: literature and research grounding; hypothesis formation and planning; experimentation and tool use; feedback, validation, and review; and reporting and knowledge communication. We further synthesize AI scientist systems, mixed-initiative co-research frameworks, benchmarks, domain deployments, and open-source infrastructures. Finally, we propose five evaluation dimensions--novelty, validity, impact, reliability, and provenance--and show that AutoResearch autonomy is domain-conditioned, being more credible in structured, executable, and rapidly verifiable settings but limited in embodied, delayed, heterogeneous, ethical, or institutionally accountable contexts.

</details>


### [216] [GENSTRAT: Toward a Science of Strategic Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23238)
*Vartan Shadarevian,Kia Ghods,Alex Kenich,Anany Kotawala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GENSTRAT通过程序化生成双人零和、不完全信息的纸牌游戏分布，结合六维能力画像和锯齿度测量，对LLM的博弈行为进行抗污染、可泛化的评估。在9个模型、超3.6万场对战中，前沿模型平均更强，但能力画像揭示了同强度模型的定性差异，锯齿度诊断出排名相近模型在局部稳定性上的显著区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有策略推理基准依赖固定规范博弈，易随模型能力提升而饱和，且无法帮助评估者从基准性能可靠地推断模型在真实、多变、混乱的部署环境中的表现。

Method: 提出GENSTRAT框架：1）程序化生成双人零和、不完美信息纸牌游戏分布，支持按需采样新游戏，实现 evergreen 评估与抵抗数据污染；2）设计六维能力画像（状态空间、时间深度、信息敏感性、对手建模、风险和脆弱性）；3）引入锯齿度（jaggedness）度量，检测模型在策略相似游戏间优势的不平滑跳跃。从2000个游戏池中采样50个基准游戏，对9个前沿和开源LLM进行超过3.6万场头对头锦标赛。

Result: 较新的前沿模型平均得分更高；整体实力相近的模型展现出质性不同的能力画像；排名前三的模型中，gpt-5和claude在局部波动性上显著高于gemini-3.1-pro，尽管整体实力接近。

Conclusion: 能力画像和锯齿度度量提供了排名本身无法提供的部署相关诊断信息，有助于更全面地评估模型在实际博弈环境中的适用性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as economic agents in marketplaces, auctions, and bidding settings. Anticipating their behavior in any specific deployment is hard. Existing strategic-reasoning benchmarks evaluate models on fixed canonical games. These benchmarks may saturate as the frontier improves, and they do not allow evaluators to generalize with confidence from benchmark performance to the varied and messy strategic environments that actual deployments involve. We introduce GENSTRAT, which uses procedurally generated strategic environments to address these challenges. Concretely, we generate a distribution of two-player zero-sum imperfect-information card games. The generator can draw fresh games on demand, allowing for evergreen evaluation and resistance to contamination. We pair the game distribution with a capability-profile methodology that decomposes model competence across six axes (state space, temporal depth, information sensitivity, opponent modeling, risk, and brittleness). We also introduce a jaggedness measure of within-distribution smoothness that detects when a model's advantage jumps unpredictably between strategically similar games. We sample 50 benchmark games from a 2,000-game generated pool and evaluate nine frontier and open-weight LLMs in a head-to-head tournament with over 36,000 matches. Newer frontier-tier models score higher on average. Beyond that average, models with near-identical overall strength show qualitatively different capability profiles, and two of the top three leaderboard models (gpt-5 and claude) are noticeably more locally volatile than the third (gemini-3.1-pro), despite being close in overall strength. Together, the capability profile and the jaggedness measure give a deployment-relevant diagnostic that the overall ranking alone cannot provide.

</details>


### [217] [Ontological Knowledge Blocks: Executable Compliance and Profile-Based Validation for Trustworthy AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23297)
*Aasish Kumar Sharma,Julian M. Kunkel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出本体知识块（OKB），一种可编程治理基础设施，将法规义务编译为机器可检查的约束，通过5元组形式化绑定规范义务与RDF/OWL概念模式、SHACL验证规则、证据要求和PROV-O溯源链接。在AI辅助HPC资源分配场景中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI治理合规依赖文档中心方法（散文描述、静态检查表、人工审查），无法规模化适用于自动化AI系统。

Method: 形式化定义OKB为5元组，包含RDF/OWL概念模式、SHACL验证规则、证据要求、PROV-O溯源链接；设计确定性法规编译器将IR记录转换为可组合的知识库模块。

Result: 24次验证运行、4个治理配置下，SHACL验证延迟12.6-100.3ms，展示配置敏感验证、严格累加违规检测，且组合配置为最全面配置。

Conclusion: OKB提供了一种可编程、可扩展的AI治理合规基础设施，支持无修改服务代码的配置重配置，所有实现已开源。

Abstract: AI-enabled services deployed in critical digital infrastructure are subject to governance obligations spanning transparency, accountability, fairness, and traceability. Compliance today remains documentation-centric: obligations are described in prose, audits rely on static checklists, and verification depends on manual review. Such approaches do not scale to automated AI systems. This paper introduces Ontological Knowledge Blocks (OKBs), a programmable governance infrastructure that compiles regulatory obligations into machine-checkable constraints over structured evidence graphs. We formalize an OKB as a 5-tuple that binds normative obligations to an RDF/OWL concept schema, executable SHACL validation rules, explicit evidence requirements, and PROV-O provenance links. A deterministic regulatory compiler translates structured Intermediate Representation (IR) records into composable KB modules, enabling profile-based governance reconfiguration without modifying service code. We implement two prototypes and evaluate them in an AI-assisted HPC resource allocation scenario across 24 validation runs and four governance profiles. Results demonstrate profile-sensitive validation, strictly additive violation accumulation, SHACL validation latency between 12.6 ms and 100.3 ms, and profile equivalence testing confirming Combined as the strictly most comprehensive profile. All artefacts are released as open source.

</details>


### [218] [DART: Semantic Recoverability for Structured Tool Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23311)
*Ke Yang,Panpan Li,Zonghan Wu,Kejin Xu,Huaxi Huang,Xiaoshui Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DART形式化了"语义可恢复性"概念，通过检查点对齐与可接受性判定机制，在结构化工具智能体执行失败时实现既避免完全重放又保证下游已提交工作语义一致性的局部恢复，实验证明其正确恢复了所有基线失败案例且未产生不安全回滚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化工具智能体在中间执行失败时，现有方案存在两难：完全重放安全但浪费资源，局部检查点恢复高效但可能导致下游已提交工作与不存在的上游历史不一致。在承诺敏感场景中，这个矛盾尤为突出。

Method: 形式化定义语义可恢复性，设计DART模块化运行时，包含四个步骤：定位失败实例、认证该实例的语义可恢复边界、将检查点对齐到这些边界、在依赖和效应约束下选择可接受恢复点以保留已提交下游工作，否则阻止回滚。

Result: 在三个LLM驱动领域和基于LangGraph的外部验证中，DART正确恢复了所有基线局部恢复失败的承诺敏感案例；在五个领域的安全审计中未发现任何不安全回滚。

Conclusion: 控制器合法性不等同于语义有效性，合理的局部恢复需要显式的可接受性检查。这填补了现有恢复方法缺乏语义有效性判据的理论空白。

Abstract: When a structured tool agent fails mid-execution, the runtime faces a dilemma: replaying the entire task is safe but wasteful, while restoring from a local checkpoint is efficient but can leave committed downstream work tied to an upstream history that no longer exists. This tension is acute in commitment-sensitive settings, where rollback targets a single failed instance yet downstream consumers have already acted on its output. Existing recovery approaches provide mechanical rollback but no criterion for whether a local restore remains semantically valid after downstream commitment. We formalize this gap as semantic recoverability and address it in DART, a modular runtime that localizes the failed instance, certifies semantically recoverable boundaries of that instance, aligns checkpoints to those boundaries, and selects an admissible restore point that preserves committed downstream work under dependency and effect constraints-or blocks otherwise. Across three LLM-driven domains and external validation on a LangGraph-based substrate, DART correctly recovers all evaluated commitment-sensitive cases where baseline local recovery fails, and a five-domain safety audit finds no unsafe admitted rollbacks. These results show that controller legality does not imply semantic validity, and that sound local recovery requires an explicit admissibility check.

</details>


### [219] [Human-in-the-Loop Multi-Agent Ventilator Decision Support with Contextual Bandit Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23320)
*Sijia Li,Xiaoyu Tan,Qixing Wang,Weiyi Zhao,Chen Zhan,Teqi Hao,Xuemin Wang,Lei Gu,Roland Eils,Xihe Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VDSS是一个多智能体人机协作框架，通过合同驱动的结构化接口协调模块化决策组件，利用上下文赌博机在线适应临床医生偏好，并通过结构化拒绝反馈触发目标重规划，实现可追溯、可控的呼吸机决策支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有呼吸机决策支持方法（基于规则、端到端强化学习、大语言模型系统）难以实现个性化泛化、控制与审计。

Method: 提出VDSS框架：多智能体协调+合同驱动接口+上下文赌博机在线偏好适应+结构化拒绝反馈重规划。

Result: ICU轨迹回放与专家评估显示VDSS推荐接受度更高，达成可接受计划所需的交互轮次更少。

Conclusion: VDSS支持临床可部署的人机协作，提供可审计的决策证据。

Abstract: Ventilator decision support requires sequential decisions that track evolving physiology and disease trajectories while respecting safety boundaries and clinician specific tuning styles. Rule based approaches rarely generalize personalization, and end to end reinforcement learning or single large language model systems remain difficult to control and audit. We propose the Ventilator Decision Support System (VDSS), a human in the loop multi agent framework that coordinates modular decision components through contract driven structured interfaces and produces traceable evidence for review. VDSS performs online preference adaptation with a contextual bandit, updating clinician specific preferences from the final accepted decision at each adjustment cycle and using them to guide subsequent recommendations. Structured rejection feedback triggers targeted replanning to reduce unproductive iterations and improve interaction stability. Retrospective ICU trajectory replay with expert review indicates higher recommendation acceptability and fewer interaction rounds to reach an acceptable plan, supporting clinically deployable human AI collaboration.

</details>


### [220] [When Planning Fails Despite Correct Execution: On Epistemic Calibration for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23414)
*Zehao Wang,Shilong Jin,Zhao Cao,Lanjun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于信息一致性的规划校准框架(EPC-AW)，通过跨智能体评估规划在不同信息条件下的稳定性来解决多智能体系统中的认知校准偏差问题，在多个任务上平均提升9.75%的系统级成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多智能体系统在执行层面容易发现错误，但智能体在规划阶段可能错误判断自身知识边界（认知校准偏差），这种偏差在规划时是潜在且动态的，难以被现有方法检测和纠正。

Method: 提出EPC-AW工作流，包含两个核心模块：(1)基于信息一致性的规划选择——跨智能体评估规划在不同信息条件下的稳定性；(2)基于一致性的认知状态精炼——利用历史偏差自适应调整校准过程，指导未来规划。

Result: 在多个任务上平均提升9.75%的系统级成功率，表明该方法能有效缓解认知校准偏差。

Conclusion: 通过关注信息条件变化下规划支持的稳定性而非直接验证可行性，EPC-AW为多智能体系统的规划校准问题提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems can fail even when planned actions are executed correctly because agents may misjudge their knowledge when evaluating plan feasibility, a phenomenon we term epistemic miscalibration in planning. Unlike execution errors, epistemic miscalibration is latent during planning, as generated plans can remain self-consistent and executable without observable errors; the miscalibration is also dynamic, as new information can alter feasibility assessments, potentially obscuring past miscalibration signals and causing them to recur over time. To address this, we propose the Epistemic Planning Calibration Agentic Workflow (EPC-AW), which assesses whether plans remain supported under varying information conditions rather than directly verifying feasibility. EPC-AW employs Information-consistency-based Plan Selection, selecting plans whose evaluations are stable across agents, together with Consistency-guided Epistemic State Refinement to adapt calibration over time by leveraging past discrepancies to guide future planning. Experiments show that EPC-AW improves system-level success by an average of 9.75%.

</details>


### [221] [EDGE-OPD: Internalizing Privileged Context with Evidence Guided On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23493)
*Aristotelis Lazaridis,Dylan Bates,Aman Sharma,Brian King,Vincent Lu,Jack FitzGerald*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这篇论文研究了在线策略自我蒸馏（OPSD）在LLM后训练中的问题——特权上下文会导致模型行为偏移（如改变推理、降低通用能力）。提出EDGE-OPD方法，通过引导式采样注入特权行为，并仅对特权上下文支持的token位置进行更新（证据掩码），解决了OPSD在稀有token/身份设定下完全无法学习目标行为的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD虽能利用单一模型作为师生、并在训练阶段注入推理时缺失的特权上下文（如人格、私密事实），但特权信息会导致模型行为过度改变（修改推理、降低通用能力、改变长度/风格等副作用），使得学生模型学到的是非预期的副作用而非可迁移的目标行为。

Method: 提出EDGE-OPD，包含两个关键设计：a) 引导式采样（guided rollouts）：在采样时向学生注入特权上下文行为，使稀有目标行为出现在在线策略数据中；b) 证据掩码（evidence mask）：仅更新特权上下文支持当前采样token的那些位置，而非rollout中的每个token。

Result: 实验证明，OPSD及其变体RLSD（无论有无验证器）在身份学习任务上完全失败，而加入引导式采样后能成功；掩码区域消融实验表明人格信号定位在正证据尾部，能实现高效知识迁移并保持通用能力。

Conclusion: EDGE-OPD通过引导式采样和证据掩码解决了OPSD在特权上下文场景下的行为偏移问题，能有效学习目标身份而不破坏通用能力，为高效知识迁移提供了新思路。
